<a name="bookmark=id.tnyz25kjqi6f"></a><a name="bookmark=id.wm0mi4y7z1cp"></a>**THE ÆTHERIUM CODEX**

**UNIFIED ENGINE**

Operating Philosophy

*The narrative engine, the game system, and the expression layer are one architecture in three vocabularies*
# **THE CORE INSIGHT**
There are not three systems. There is one system described three ways.

The narrative engine generates situations through profiling, pressure extraction, front organization, clock tracking, symptom placement, observation collapse, and feedback compounding. The game system resolves situations through Condition Tracks, Composite Defense, Soak, the Spell Sentence, talent trees, Fulcrum Points, and the four-pillar resource model. The expression layer presents situations through attention direction, temporal texture, information sequencing, evidentiary presentation, embodied expression, serial packaging, and persistence encoding.

These are not three layers stacked on top of each other. They are three readings of the same architecture. The Spell Sentence IS the symptom vocabulary. Condition Tracks ARE clocks. The Legacy System IS the feedback loop. The Psyche Schema IS the profiling instrument. The Political Zoetrope IS the civilizational front system. The isotope map IS the resource and expression layer simultaneously. There are no analogies in the preceding sentences. Each IS statement is an identity statement.
# **THE SEVEN VERTEBRAE**
The spine is a cyclic sequence of seven operations. Each cycle’s output feeds the next cycle’s input. The accumulation across cycles IS the campaign, the novel, the community, or the creative project.

|**#**|**Vertebra**|**Function**|**Game System Identity**|**Expression Bond**|
| :- | :- | :- | :- | :- |
|**1**|**Schema**|Profile the entity. Map identity, values, contradictions, narrative type.|Psyche Schema, Archetypes, College Affiliation, Soul Stone, Legacy System|Persistence Encoding|
|**2**|**Pressure Points**|Extract where energy concentrates. Ten Questions + three Schema diagnostic reads.|Condition Tracks, Poise/Clout, Composite Defense, Derived Stats|Evidentiary Presentation|
|**3**|**Fronts**|Organize actors. Map relationships. Derive the Default Timeline.|Political Zoetrope, College System, Spell Sentence as institutional action, Dual Periodic Table|Information Sequencing|
|**4**|**Clocks**|Track advancement. Mark thresholds. Generate bangs.|Condition Tracks AS Clocks, Fulcrum Points, Scene/Sequel as action economy|Temporal Texture + Collins|
|**5**|**Symptoms**|Place seeds. Hold in superposition. Ensure discovery redundancy.|Spell Sentence as symptom vocabulary, Isotope specificity, Five Alchemy Layers|Attention Direction|
|**6**|**Adventures**|Collapse observation. Resolve engagement. Produce consequences.|Combat/Skill/Social resolution, Talent Trees, Spell Sentence as participant action|Embodied Expression|
|**7**|**Feedback Loop**|Cascade consequences. Read pattern. Crystallize meaning.|Legacy System, XP/Mastery/ESL, Soul Stone as feedback medium, Positional Divinity|Serial Packaging|
# **THE THREE LENSES**
The same events are legible through three perspectives. Each perspective is correct. Each is incomplete without the other two.

|**The GM Lens**|The prep-side view. What the operator built, what clocks are running, what fronts are acting, what truths are available. This lens sees the machinery. It is the perspective from which the system is operated. Documents 1A through 5C primarily serve this lens.|
| :- | :- |
|**The Player Lens**|The experience-side view. What the participant encounters, investigates, debates, and chooses. This lens sees the fiction. It is the perspective from which the system is experienced. Document 6A primarily serves this lens. The player never sees the machinery. They see the output.|
|**The Retrospective Lens**|The meaning-side view. What emerged from the collision between preparation and observation. This lens sees the pattern. It is available only after the cycle completes. Documents 7A and 7B serve this lens. The meaning was not planned. It was discovered.|

The unified engine is not optimized for one lens. It produces output legible through all three simultaneously. A session that works only from the GM perspective (mechanically functional but experientially flat) is incomplete. A session that works only from the player perspective (exciting but structurally arbitrary) is incomplete. A session that produces no retrospective pattern (fun in the moment but forgotten afterward) is incomplete. The complete session is legible through all three.
# **THE UNIVERSAL CHARACTER SHEET**
Everything uses the same character sheet. The same Schema instrument profiles player characters, NPCs, institutions, territories, gods, and the Overdeity. The same rules apply. The same build rules apply. The same pressure extraction procedure applies. The same front generation procedure applies. The same clock tracking applies. The same feedback loop applies.

The difference between an NPC and an institution is not the system. It is the SCALE. The same Spell Sentence effect describes a character casting a spell and an institution deploying a program. The same clock template tracks a character’s wound recovery and a civilization’s governmental transition. The same Legacy System reads pattern in a character’s accumulated choices and in a territory’s accumulated factional resolutions.

The perception of three separate game modes (character-scale, institutional-scale, civilizational-scale) is a filter across the same medium. The Fulcrum Point distance model makes this explicit: the participant extends their reach from Distance 0 (personal resolution) through Distance 3 (institutional manipulation) to Distance ∞ (cosmological intervention) on a single continuum. No game mode changes. The reach changes. The content changes. The system is the same.
# **THE OPERATING PRINCIPLES**
Nine principles that govern how the engine is used. These emerged from the build process and are carried forward as design law.

|**1. The story is in the situation, not the sequence**|Prepare density, not direction. The GM writes truths, not plots. The participants determine which truths they discover and in what order. Every path through the density produces a coherent story because the truths are internally consistent.|
| :- | :- |
|**2. Complexity generates energy**|Character depth IS mechanical power. The pressure point count that generates interesting story is the same count that generates Fulcrum Points. A shallow character produces few FP and few interesting situations. A contradictory character produces many of both. The system rewards complexity.|
|**3. Observation creates reality**|Seeds exist in superposition until the participant observes them. The GM holds multiple possible truths simultaneously. The participant’s investigation determines which one was always real. This is the Palimpsest principle made operational. Schrödinger’s Gun is not a trick. It is the setting’s physics expressed through the GM’s methodology.|
|**4. Meaning is retrospective**|The Selûnae read fate backward. The Legacy System reads character identity backward. The retrospective lens reads the campaign’s theme backward. No meaning is authored in advance. All meaning is discovered after the fact by participants who lived through the events. The campfire’s product belongs to everyone around it.|
|**5. The spine is a cycle, not a line**|Every output feeds the next input. The feedback loop is not an optional appendix. It is the mechanism that makes session 30 different from session 1. Without compounding, the game is a series of isolated events. With compounding, the game is a campaign.|
|**6. Change must be earned**|The Smoothing Effect ensures that only sustained pressure from genuine tensions produces lasting change. Easy change is meaningless. Resisted change is dramatic. Clocks have segments because the world resists being altered. The resistance makes the alteration significant when it finally arrives.|
|**7. The operator must be invisible**|The catalyst initiates the reaction without becoming part of the product. The Gray Man principle ensures that the campfire belongs to the group, not to the person who lit it. Visibility collapses participation into consumption.|
|**8. Every entity uses the same rules**|NPCs are not exempt from the physics that govern PCs. Institutions are not exempt from the physics that govern NPCs. Gods are not exempt from the physics that govern institutions. Scale changes. Rules do not. Aíon is not an exception — Aíon is the largest-scale expression of the same system.|
|**9. Bucket filling precedes design decisions**|Do not propose where mechanics could go until all relevant source material has been catalogued. Do not lock decisions until the data supports them. The extraction procedure at Vertebra 2 IS this principle applied to NPC generation. The same principle applies to the engine’s own development.|
# **THE DISTANCE PRINCIPLE**
Everything the participant can do is describable as a Distance operation on the spine. The cost increases with distance. The side effects increase with distance. The narrative impact increases with distance. One currency, one mechanic, one spine, different reach.

|**Dist**|**Cost**|**Target**|**Play Mode**|**Side Effect Scale**|
| :- | :- | :- | :- | :- |
|**0**|**1 FP**|Current vertebra|Combat, immediate action|Minimal — within current frame|
|**1**|**2 FP**|Adjacent vertebra|Social encounters, exploration, seed placement|Minor — one skipped vertebra processes later|
|**2**|**3 FP**|Two vertebrae away|Clock manipulation, pressure point revelation|Moderate — front acts on outdated information|
|**3**|**4 FP**|Three vertebrae away|Relationship edge alteration, Schema element reveal|Significant — network rebalances around altered edge|
|**∞**|**5 FP**|Regulatory systems|Smoothing override, cosmological intervention|Maximum — Aíon’s architecture notices and responds|
# **THE COSMOLOGICAL GROUNDING**
The engine’s architecture is not a game designer’s abstraction imposed on a setting. It is the setting’s cosmology expressed as playable mechanics.

The Palimpsest holds superposition states. Fulcrum Points are the participant’s ability to choose which state collapses. The Smoothing Effect is Aíon continuously spending infinite FP to maintain coherence across two merged realities. The five alchemy layers are five planes’ isotopes expressing through observable effects. The Spell Sentence is the grammar of those effects at every scale. The Legacy System is the Selûnae reading fate backward. The Zoetrope is the civilizational clock operating at the longest timescale.

When the participant spends Fulcrum Points, they are performing a cosmological act. They are manipulating the Palimpsest. When the GM holds seeds in superposition, they are performing Aíon’s function at the table scale. When the table reads the campaign’s meaning retrospectively, they are performing the Selûnae’s function at the human scale. The game’s cosmology and the game’s mechanics are the same system. The setting’s gods and the table’s participants are performing the same operations at different scales. This is not metaphor. It is architecture.
# **THE REGULATORY SYSTEMS**
Eight systems that shape how every vertebra processes its content. Two diagnostics, one method, five governors.

|**System**|**Type**|**One-Line Function**|
| :- | :- | :- |
|**Information/Choice/Consequence**|*Diagnostic*|If it stalls, one of three elements is missing. Supply it.|
|**Red Team**|*Method*|Adopt the opposing perspective to validate your work.|
|**Schema Co-Completion**|*Governor*|Optimal specification leaves room for participant contribution.|
|**Transportation**|*Governor*|Protect the fictional dream above structural cleverness.|
|**Smoothing Effect**|*Governor*|Resist dramatic change. Reward sustained pressure.|
|**Three Survival Functions**|*Validator*|Every cycle needs encoding, calibration, and binding.|
|**Information Economy**|*Governor*|Control flow speed through supply, demand, and cost.|
|**Interaction Ritual**|*Prerequisite*|Four conditions for emotional peak: focus, mood, awareness, boundary.|
# **THE CAMPFIRE PRINCIPLE**
The unified engine is a campfire. The operator lights it and steps back. The participants gather around it and co-create. The fire’s fuel is prepared density — truths held in superposition. The fire’s heat is emotional engagement produced when Collins’ conditions are met. The fire’s light is meaning — the retrospective pattern that emerges when the table looks at the path they’ve walked together.

The campfire is not a metaphor for the engine. The campfire IS what the engine produces. The three survival functions (information encoding, social norm calibration, group binding) are not aspirational goals. They are the outputs of a properly operating spine cycle. A cycle that produces all three is a complete session. A cycle that produces fewer is mechanically functional but humanly incomplete.

The engine’s ultimate product is not a story. It is a shared experience that the participants remember, reference, and use to understand themselves and each other. The story is the residue. The experience is the product. The meaning is the gift the participants give each other by tending the fire together.
# **THE DOCUMENT MAP**
Twenty-one documents specifying the complete architecture.

|**Phase**|**Document**|**Content**|
| :- | :- | :- |
|**1**|**0 — Operating Philosophy**|This document. The architecture’s foundational principles, read last, written last.|
|**1**|**1A — Psyche Schema v4**|The profiling instrument. 10-band overlay, three sections, companion elements, FP generation.|

*The spine is specified. The documents are built. The architecture is one system in three vocabularies, cyclic, self-correcting, scale-independent, and cosmologically grounded. The next phase is playtesting — running it at a table to calibrate the values, validate the procedures, and discover what the architecture produces when real participants collapse its superpositions into real, shared, remembered experience.*

## <a name="_heading=h.72phrvf3hoe4"></a>**Document 1: One Engine, Different Scales, Same Dials**
### <a name="_heading=h.h1y0bx3f5en2"></a>**Alpha Draft — April 15, 2026**

# <a name="_heading=h.t7ye9seez1z"></a>**PART I: DESIGN PRINCIPLES**
## <a name="_heading=h.l3qvykz4j9ym"></a>**The Core Assertion**
Every system in the Ætherium Codex is an implementation of one underlying engine. Combat, social interaction, crafting, institutional operations, environmental phenomena, civilizational drift, and cosmological maintenance all use the same structural components operating at different scales. No system is an exception. No system requires its own rules. The components are:

1\.          **Scope** — the scale at which an action operates

2\.          **Pillars** — the axes along which health, damage, and consequence are tracked

3\.          **Petals** — the domains of trained capability that characters use to act

4\.          **Tempo** — the unit of action within a given Scope level

5\.          **Clocks** — the mechanism by which accumulated small changes produce large-scale state transitions

6\.          **Debt** — the universal cost model for carrying conditions, adaptations, and consequences

These six components combine to describe any interaction at any scale. A sword fight and a trade war use the same components — different content, same architecture.
## <a name="_heading=h.uecwudir8iah"></a>**The Relationship Between Components**
Scope determines WHAT you’re affecting and at what temporal resolution. Pillars determine WHICH axis of the target is being tested. Petals determine WHAT training the actor brings to the action. Tempo determines HOW MUCH the actor can do within a single phrase at their operating Scope. Clocks determine HOW accumulated actions at one Scope propagate to adjacent Scopes. Debt determines HOW conditions are carried, integrated, or failed.

No component operates in isolation. Every action involves all six simultaneously. A Wildpath Warden sealing a Reality Seam is: operating at Scope 4-5 (regional/operational), testing the Metaphysical pillar of the environment, using Philosophy and Knowledge petal talents, spending tempos on channeling actions, advancing or retarding the local ley line clock, and carrying Mental debt from the college initiation that made them capable of the work.

-----
# <a name="_heading=h.qkchthp1wsla"></a>**PART II: THE SCOPE SCALE**
## <a name="_heading=h.ctvd4qfnqaba"></a>**Definition**
Scope measures two independent variables that are normally locked together but can be decoupled through infrastructure:

**Scope of Effect:** How large is the entity, area, or concept being affected? This is fixed by the target.

**Dissemination Time:** How long do effects take to propagate through the affected scope? This is variable based on communication and coordination infrastructure.

An army of 10,000 with runner communication has Scope 6 effect with Scope 6 dissemination — orders take days to propagate across the force. The same army with Forge-linked ward communication has Scope 6 effect with Scope 3 dissemination — orders arrive in minutes. The Forge infrastructure did not change what the army can affect. It changed how fast effects propagate.
## <a name="_heading=h.jp6f29h9n6f8"></a>**The Scale**

| Scope  | Temporal Resolution        | Spatial/Conceptual Reach       | What Operates Here                                                                |
| :----- | :------------------------- | :----------------------------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **S0** | Seconds                    | Personal / arm’s reach         | Individual exchange: sword fight, psychic duel, face-to-face negotiation          |
| **S1** | Seconds to minutes         | Fireteam / voice range (~30ft) | Small group coordination: covering fire, squad argument, fireteam tactics         |
| **S2** | Minutes                    | Engagement zone (~150ft)       | Skirmish, archery field, courtroom trial, item drip recharge (10-minute interval) |
| **S3** | Minutes to tens of minutes | Company / building complex     | Battle command, siege section, institutional meeting, complex channeling          |
| **S4** | Tens of minutes to hours   | Battlefield / district         | Full battle, district operation, public address, guild deployment                 |
| **S5** | Hours to days              | Fortress / town / operation    | Siege, extended operation, political crisis, faction action                       |
| **S6** | Days to weeks              | Region / campaign              | Military campaign, trade route management, faction turn                           |
| **S7** | Weeks to months            | Territory / institution        | Institutional strategy, economic policy, seasonal planning                        |
| **S8** | Months to years            | Civilization / multi-territory | Trade wars, alliances, Zoetrope pressure, cultural shifts                         |
| **S9** | Years to decades           | Epoch / setting-scale          | Civilizational drift, Smoothing maintenance, planar boundary shifts               |
| **S∞** | Cosmological               | The architecture itself        | Aíon-level operations, Smoothing override, regulatory system manipulation         |

The scale matches the 1-10 system used by Mastery, Formation Stage, and isotope ranks. Everything in the system runs on the same spine.
## <a name="_heading=h.ssq62d2k04c6"></a>**Scope and Mastery**
Mastery does NOT limit which Scope levels a character can access. A Mastery 3 militia captain IS operating at Scope 3-4 when they direct troops. They’re not good at it, but they’re doing it.

Mastery determines how EFFECTIVELY a character operates at a given Scope — their tempo budget, their action options, their ability to manage information flow across Scope boundaries. A Mastery 3 captain issuing orders at Scope 4 is slower, less precise, and more prone to information loss than a Mastery 8 general doing the same thing.
## <a name="_heading=h.69skrjcjsnb9"></a>**Scope and the Drip Recharge System**
The drip recharge system’s 10-minute base interval places it at Scope 2. This is not arbitrary — item charges, Stamina recovery, and short rest mechanics all operate at the engagement/skirmish temporal resolution. The recharge rate scales with item tier through the existing formula, but the BASE interval is anchored to Scope 2’s temporal resolution.

|Item Tier|Tick Interval|Scope Anchor|
| :- | :- | :- |
|0 (Mundane)|1 minute|S1 (fireteam)|
|1-4 (Novice)|10 minutes|S2 (engagement)|
|5-7 (Journeyman)|1 hour|S3-4 (company/battlefield)|
|8-10 (Master)|12-24 hours|S5-7 (operation/territorial)|

Mastery 10 is the absolute peak of mortal expression and performance. Beyond 10 enters the Legendary/Monstrous scale (attribute × 2) and the Divine scale (attribute × 10) — these are separate attribute tracks similar to Mega Attributes, not extensions of the mortal 1-10 scale.

Higher-tier items recharge at higher Scope temporal resolutions. A Novice item refills during a skirmish break. A Master item refills overnight or across a full day depending on Formation Stage.

# <a name="_heading=h.i6w72wlug7zn"></a>**PART III: THE FOUR PILLARS**
## <a name="_heading=h.rnld7jw5qrqw"></a>**Definition**
The four pillars are the axes along which any entity’s health, integrity, and functional capacity are measured. They operate at EVERY Scope level. The content changes with scale. The diagnostic function is identical: how healthy is this entity along this axis?
## <a name="_heading=h.h97hfypv4a8z"></a>**Physical (Structural Integrity)**
The physical composition, structural soundness, and material functionality of the entity.

|Scope|What It Tracks|Example Threats|
| :- | :- | :- |
|S0|Body: bones, organs, blood, tissue|Sword wound, blunt force, burns, falling|
|S1|Squad effectiveness: casualties, exhaustion, ammunition|Ambush, suppressive fire, booby traps|
|S2|Engagement infrastructure: fortifications, equipment, positions|Siege engines, sappers, engineering operations|
|S3-4|Operational infrastructure: supply depots, roads, facilities|Sabotage, targeted destruction, logistics disruption|
|S5-6|Regional infrastructure: trade routes, production, agriculture|Blockade, sanctions, scorched earth|
|S7-8|Civilizational infrastructure: cities, industry, transportation networks|Strategic bombing, territorial conquest, economic warfare|
|S9+|Setting-scale physical integrity|Planar boundary rupture, cosmological structural failure|
## <a name="_heading=h.ygas6dtefa7t"></a>**Metaphysical (Elemental/Chemical/Biological Integrity)**
The isotope composition, planar coherence, and fundamental chemical-biological health of the entity. This is NOT limited to “magic.” It encompasses the full spectrum of the five alchemy layers: inorganic chemistry (Borderlands), organic chemistry and biology (Verdant Font), thermodynamics and entropy (Synod), catalysis and process chemistry (Hidden Forge), and statistical mechanics and probability (Palimpsest).

Agent Orange is a Metaphysical attack — Borderlands + Verdant Font compound isotope weaponized at Scope 7-8, poisoning the chemical and biological substrate of an entire territory for generations.

|Scope|What It Tracks|Example Threats|
| :- | :- | :- |
|S0|Personal biochemistry: Strain, poison, disease, radiation|Toxic exposure, disease vector, isotope contamination|
|S1|Local environmental quality: water, air, soil, ambient isotope levels|Localized contamination, bleed zone expansion|
|S2|Engagement-zone hazards: ward stability, tactical isotope use|Ward sabotage, chemical weapon deployment|
|S3-4|Operational magical infrastructure: communication networks, ritual sites, Kan-tech systems|Infrastructure disruption, communication ward collapse|
|S5-6|Regional elemental health: ley line stability, agricultural soil quality, water table purity|Resource extraction damage, ley line destabilization, industrial pollution|
|S7-8|Civilizational elemental health: territorial isotope balance, genetic health of populations, Smoothing Effect regional strength|Weaponized isotope contamination, genetic damage, territorial planar collapse|
|S9+|Cosmological coherence: the Smoothing Effect, planar boundary integrity|Reality Seam catastrophe, Coherence Clock failure|
## <a name="_heading=h.2wiv3ua565nv"></a>**Mental (Identity Coherence)**
The psychological integrity, cognitive function, and identity coherence of the entity. At personal Scope, this is the individual’s composure, conviction, and sense of self. At institutional Scope, this is the organization’s ideological alignment and internal trust. At civilizational Scope, this is cultural identity and collective will.

|Scope|What It Tracks|Example Threats|
| :- | :- | :- |
|S0|Personal composure: will, conviction, courage, self-control|Intimidation, psychic assault, traumatic experience, belief failure|
|S1|Squad morale: unit cohesion, trust, willingness to fight|Casualties, leadership loss, betrayal within unit|
|S2|Force morale: operational belief that victory is possible|Heavy losses, failed operations, propaganda targeting troops|
|S3-4|Institutional ideology: organizational belief in mission and methods|Internal schism, revealed hypocrisy, infiltration|
|S5-6|Regional identity: community sense of shared purpose|Cultural displacement, imposed ideology, institutional collapse|
|S7-8|Civilizational identity: cultural will to exist as coherent entity|Cultural genocide, forced assimilation, narrative replacement|
|S9+|Cosmological narrative coherence: the Great Lie’s stability|Mass Cognitive Dissonance, exposure of the Great Truth at population scale|
## <a name="_heading=h.jyeafrjdaq1d"></a>**Social (Legitimacy / Standing)**
The reputation, credibility, and relational standing of the entity within its social context. At personal Scope, this is face-to-face credibility. At institutional Scope, this is political standing and alliance reliability. At civilizational Scope, this is sovereignty and international legitimacy.

|Scope|What It Tracks|Example Threats|
| :- | :- | :- |
|S0|Personal credibility: Poise, composure under social pressure|Accusation, exposure, insult, evidence of wrongdoing|
|S1|Local standing: group trust, authority within immediate community|Public accusation, witnessed failure, gossip|
|S2|Operational credibility: reputation among those you interact with|Atrocities, broken promises, collateral damage|
|S3-4|Institutional standing: political influence, alliance reliability|Broken agreements, diplomatic scandals, revealed operations|
|S5-6|Regional legitimacy: authority recognized across a territory|Failed governance, popular uprising, rival claimant|
|S7-8|Civilizational legitimacy: right to exist as a sovereign entity|War of aggression, humanitarian violations, state failure|
|S9+|Cosmological legitimacy: divine mandate, positional divinity|Loss of divine portfolio, Zoetrope transition, theological crisis|

# <a name="_heading=h.qd0tc41h6wiq"></a>**PART IV: THE FOUR PETALS**
## <a name="_heading=h.s70qk984muie"></a>**Definition**
The four petals are domains of trained capability. Each petal has a system it modifies — a mechanical engine that the petal’s talents operate on. The petals describe WHAT YOU TRAINED IN. The pillars describe WHAT YOU’RE AFFECTING. Any petal can target any pillar. There are no restrictions on petal-to-pillar crossing.

|Petal|What It Governs|System It Modifies|Infrastructure It Uses|
| :- | :- | :- | :- |
|**Combat**|How you fight, physically and tactically|Physical combat engine (tempo, engagement states, action atoms)|Weapons, armor, physical condition tracks|
|**Knowledge**|What you know, analyze, and create|Crafting/analysis engine (item creation, schematics, material science)|Materials, tools, schematics, item architecture|
|**Philosophy**|How you channel metaphysical force|Spell Sentence system (isotopes, effects, Construction Pool, ÆP)|Isotope ranks, effect tiers, ÆP pool, Mastery Capacity|
|**Social**|How you interact with people and institutions|Background system (Poise, Clout, Rank, Title, Standing Track)|Backgrounds, contacts, reputation, institutional position|
## <a name="_heading=h.3ibduiawr709"></a>**Free Crossing**
Petals are actions. Pillars are defenses. They cross freely.

A Combat petal talent can target any pillar: - Combat → Physical: a Power Strike targeting Guard - Combat → Mental: an Intimidation check mid-combat targeting Courage - Combat → Social: a dramatic victory affecting the enemy commander’s Standing - Combat → Metaphysical: a weapon strike disrupting an opponent’s isotope expression

A Social petal talent can target any pillar: - Social → Social: a diplomatic argument targeting Dignity - Social → Mental: a psychological manipulation targeting Conviction - Social → Physical: a Commander ordering a charge (Social talent producing Physical consequence through institutional authority) - Social → Metaphysical: a political decision to contaminate a region’s water supply (Social action producing Metaphysical consequence)

The petal determines the actor’s training and approach. The pillar determines which of the target’s defenses responds. The Scope determines the scale and temporal resolution.

—
# <a name="_heading=h.9inh24xst87s"></a>**THE ÆTHERIUM CODEX — SKILL × EFFECT COMPATIBILITY MATRIX**
## <a name="_heading=h.m3sy8o95vfvh"></a>**Raw Bucket Assignment — Can This Skill Produce This Effect?**
### <a name="_heading=h.eigm2sij9cbl"></a>**Working Document — April 16, 2026**
-----
## <a name="_heading=h.pu6vbapd16cu"></a>**METHOD**
Each cell answers ONE question: can a trained practitioner using this skill produce this effect through technique alone (Tier 1-2), without constructing a Spell Sentence (Tier 3)?

**✓** = Yes, this skill can produce this effect through trained application. **—** = No, this skill cannot produce this effect.

No hierarchy. No primary/secondary. Just compatibility.

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.ga36z5pd09im"></a>**THE MATRIX**

## <a name="_heading=h.tlyrtc7obmuk"></a>**OBSERVATIONS**
### <a name="_heading=h.5fw8t3jw5y2b"></a>**Operate Is the Universal Interface**
Operate scores 23 of 24 (only missing Feature). This is correct — Operate doesn’t produce effects, it ACTIVATES DEVICES that produce effects. It’s the mundane Variable. The skill’s versatility IS the point. A character with high Operate and access to diverse Kan-tech can do almost anything — but they’re always dependent on having the right device.
### <a name="_heading=h.w1bbzy4auw1i"></a>**Insight Is the Narrowest Skill**
Insight scores 2 of 24 (Senses and Protection). It does ONE thing: perceive the metaphysical. Its narrowness is a feature — it’s the preternatural sense that feeds data to Metaphysics for interpretation. Breadth would dilute its identity.
### <a name="_heading=h.dgq45ltalbsi"></a>**Discovery Is Nearly As Narrow**
Discovery scores 4 of 24 (Nullify, Weaken, Senses, and nothing else). Pure perception and analysis. Finding the linchpin. It’s the Mental equivalent of Insight — narrow by design, powerful in its lane.
### <a name="_heading=h.uebap4bhj5tm"></a>**Medicine and Artifice Are the Broadest Mental Skills**
Both score 14 of 24. Medicine covers biological systems comprehensively (damage through anatomy, healing, conditions, immunity through vaccination, time manipulation through metabolic control, transformation through surgery). Artifice covers mechanical/structural systems equivalently. They’re mirrors of each other — one for organisms, one for objects.
### <a name="_heading=h.5lp44251g3m"></a>**Performance Is the Broadest Social Skill**
Performance scores 13 of 24. It covers deception, crowd manipulation, morale, pacing, artistic creation, counter-narrative, and emotional resonance. This matches the setting — Unity Academy and Chiaroscuro Institute both weaponize Performance extensively.
### <a name="_heading=h.63qugmfx337f"></a>**The Six Universal Effects Show Their Universality**

|Effect|Skills That Can Produce It|
| :- | :- |
|Senses|12 of 16|
|Protection|13 of 16|
|Damage|11 of 16|
|Affliction|11 of 16|
|Weaken|12 of 16|
|Nullify|12 of 16|

These six appear across nearly every skill. This matches the SRD v3’s designation of Affliction, Damage, Movement, Protection, Senses, and Transform as “natural to all five planes.” The skill system mirrors the planar universality at the mundane level.
### <a name="_heading=h.cvd18yphrn94"></a>**Effects That Almost No Skill Produces**

|Effect|Skills That Can Produce It|
| :- | :- |
|Feature|0 (automatic, not skill-produced)|
|Immunity|3 (Medicine, Operate, Praxis)|
|Immortality|3 (Operate, Praxis, Conveyance via vehicle)|
|Time Travel|3 (Conveyance, Operate — both via vehicles/devices)|
|Dimensional Travel|3 (Conveyance, Operate — both via vehicles/devices)|
|Mind Link|3 (Performance, Influence, Operate)|
|Variable|1 (Operate)|

The rarest effects are the most powerful and exotic ones — requiring either specialized equipment (Operate/Conveyance interfacing with devices) or deep metaphysical mastery (Praxis). This is correct — mortals don’t casually achieve immortality or time travel through basic training.

# <a name="_heading=h.5ultu8kmt1mw"></a>**PART V: THE UNIVERSAL DEBT MODEL**
## <a name="_heading=h.tgiz0vu0fvn"></a>**The Core Mechanism**
Every lasting condition — physical injury, psychological transformation, isotope mutation, social disgrace — is tracked through a single universal debt system. The architecture is identical across all four pillars. The content differs. The thresholds, zones, repayment mechanics, and failure states are the same.
## <a name="_heading=h.4w0x18xudfi0"></a>**The Thresholds**
The thresholds are calculated from the character’s **total lifetime XP** — including the equivalent XP value of creation choices (starting dots, backgrounds, Heritage States, etc.).

|Zone|Debt Range|Status|Consequences|
| :- | :- | :- | :- |
|**Safe**|Debt ≤ Lifetime XP × 2|Clean integration|Benefits/adaptation only. No penalties. The condition exists but doesn’t impair. The character has grown around it.|
|**Stressed**|Debt > Lifetime XP × 2, ≤ Lifetime XP × 8|Strained integration|Detriments manifest alongside benefits/adaptations. The character is carrying more than their development cleanly supports. Triggers fire. Reactions manifest. But the person is present and processing.|
|**Overwhelmed**|Debt > Lifetime XP × 8|Irrecoverable failure|The pillar is in catastrophic failure. Normal integration cannot recover this. Requires REMAKING, not repairing.|
## <a name="_heading=h.c95496y6a6xn"></a>**The Origin of ×2 and ×8**
**The ×2 threshold** derives from Adaptation Pressure = Source Formation Stage × 2. It is the baseline calculation for how much pressure a single exposure event creates. Below ×2, the character’s total development can absorb the condition without distortion — the adaptation is proportionally small relative to who they are.

**The ×8 threshold** is 4× the Safe threshold. The Stressed zone between ×2 and ×8 is the working zone — the character is struggling with detriments but remains present, functional, and capable of growth. Past ×8, the process has consumed the pilot.
## <a name="_heading=h.a89o1v8rd6fa"></a>**Automatic Repayment**
When any pillar’s debt exceeds the ×2 Safe threshold:

**25% of all XP gained is automatically deducted and applied to that pillar’s debt.** The character’s growth is being consumed by integration pressure. This is not optional — the body, mind, social context, or metaphysical composition is directing resources toward integrating the change.

**If multiple pillars are in debt simultaneously,** each draws its 25% independently. A character Stressed on both Physical and Mental has 50% of incoming XP consumed by auto-repayment. This creates meaningful pressure to manage debt across pillars rather than accumulating conditions freely.

**Minimum floor:** There is a guaranteed minimum XP the player receives per session regardless of total debt across all pillars. The player always has some agency over their character’s development. Adaptation pressure cannot consume everything. The pilot always gets fuel.
## <a name="_heading=h.orce4o8xch4c"></a>**Natural Threshold Growth**
Because the thresholds are based on **current lifetime XP**, simply gaining XP over time moves the ×2 and ×8 lines upward. A condition that pushed you into the Stressed zone at Mastery 3 might fall into the Safe zone by Mastery 5 — not because you paid anything toward it, but because your total development grew enough that the condition is proportionally smaller relative to who you are now.

**You grew around it.** The fire adaptation that was destabilizing at Mastery 3 is just part of you at Mastery 6. The trauma that was overwhelming at Mastery 4 is integrated background at Mastery 7. The social disgrace that consumed your identity at Mastery 3 is an old scar at Mastery 8.

This means time and experience are themselves healing mechanisms. Not because the condition changed, but because the PERSON grew.
## <a name="_heading=h.w9hrc120f9s3"></a>**Voluntary Investment**
A character can voluntarily invest XP into any pillar’s debt at any time. This accelerates integration beyond the 25% automatic deduction.
## <a name="_heading=h.cb0q6sr32m9l"></a>**Detriments**
Each detriment that manifests in the Stressed zone has its own XP cost. Detriments are tracked separately in the debt ledger.

**Paying off a detriment ELIMINATES it.** Unlike the condition itself (which stays when paid), the negative expression disappears when its debt is cleared. You outgrew the problem.

The person who “no longer flinches” has paid off the detriment of their trauma. The trauma is still THERE — the experience is part of them, the scar exists. But the flinch — the involuntary reaction, the avoidance behavior, the triggered response — is gone because they invested the growth required to overcome it. The momentary flicker of recognition still fires. The reaction that follows is managed so smoothly it never reaches the surface.

Detriments can be bought down independently. A character might choose to pay off a specific detriment first (eliminating the most disruptive penalty) while still carrying debt on the condition overall.
## <a name="_heading=h.oodfq6e5k67s"></a>**Calcification (Integration Complete)**
When all debt on a specific condition is cleared — both the condition and all its detriments — the condition **Calcifies.** The Heritage State shifts from Active to Settled.

The condition is permanent baseline. It costs nothing ongoing. It’s just what you are. You don’t maintain it. You don’t pay for it. It’s integrated.

**Calcification does NOT remove the condition.** The power, adaptation, or scar stays. The obligation goes. The Wildpath Warden’s biological adaptations are permanent. The SERE graduate’s stress inoculation is permanent. The social Title’s settled connotation is permanent. The isotope mutation is permanent. These are who you ARE, not what happened TO you.
## <a name="_heading=h.xawz9q3n98yx"></a>**Irrecoverable Failure (>×8)**
Past the ×8 threshold, normal integration cannot recover the pillar. The condition has exceeded the character’s capacity to grow around it. The process consumed the pilot.

|Pillar|What >×8 Looks Like|What Remaking Requires|
| :- | :- | :- |
|**Physical**|Accumulated structural damage exceeding integration capacity. The body is failing systemically.|Full biological reconstruction: Panacea ESL 9-10. Not healing — REBUILDING from the body’s pattern. The body that emerges is not the body that went in.|
|**Mental**|Identity coherence gone. Functioning without presence. The college failure states: clinical machine without empathy, cognitive engine without a pilot, ecosystem without direction.|Identity reconstruction. Not therapy — BUILDING a new self from whatever fragments remain. The person that emerges is not the person that went in. This is cognitive resurrection.|
|**Social**|Total social death. Vilified at a scale that normal social effort cannot reach. No community will accept the entity as it currently exists.|New identity (Constructed Identity), institutional rehabilitation at college scale, or relocation to a community where the damage hasn’t propagated. The old social self is dead. A new one must be built.|
|**Metaphysical**|Monstrous transformation. Mutations expressing uncontrollably. The body has become something inhuman. The biological engine without a pilot.|Alchemical reconstruction: Decomposition + Displacement of overwhelming mutations. Strip the expressions and rebuild from substrate. The creature that emerges is not what went in.|

All four follow the same principle: past ×8, you cannot FIX it. You REMAKE it. And the remade version is not the original restored. It is something new built from what survived.
## <a name="_heading=h.vwmq3lx4f8bs"></a>**Applied to Each Pillar — Content Summary**
**Physical Debt:** Conditions: Lasting injuries (Scarred through Terminal). Debt is the structural damage’s integration cost. Detriments: functional limitations, pain, reduced capability in specific contexts. Calcification: the injury is permanent but fully adapted to. The blind swordsman. The one-armed smith. The person who no longer flinches from the old wound.

**Mental Debt:** Conditions: Psychological transformations (trauma, college initiation, Cognitive Dissonance, belief failure). Debt is the identity integration cost. Detriments: triggers, avoidance behaviors, Schema distortion, reduced function in contexts related to the source. Calcification: the experience is permanent but fully integrated. The SERE graduate. The Wildpath diamond identity. The person who carries the weight and still tastes the coffee.

**Social Debt:** Conditions: Standing damage (reputation destruction, institutional disgrace, Title mutation). Debt is the social recovery cost. Detriments: closed doors, withheld trust, denied opportunities, mutated Titles. Calcification: the reputation is settled — whether redeemed or accepted in its mutated form. The Title stabilizes.

**Metaphysical Debt:** Conditions: Isotope mutations (Strain → Nascent → Calcification pipeline). Debt is the adaptation cost. Detriments: negative expressions of the same isotope (vulnerability, uncontrolled expression, biological side effects). Calcification: the mutation is permanent baseline. Heritage State shifts to Settled. Just what you are.
