# <a name="_heading=h.yv1fdsbvr8cr"></a>**THE ÆTHERIUM CODEX — THE COMBAT PETAL**
## <a name="_heading=h.6l3iipqp1kgu"></a>**Document 4: Talent Trees as Transmission Specifications**
### <a name="_heading=h.q4x92l4cz93d"></a>**Canonical Draft — April 16, 2026**
-----
## <a name="_heading=h.hla6e9qnhpmr"></a>**PREAMBLE**
Documents 1-3 established that every action is an effect from the 23+Variable canonical list, delivered through a transmission medium. The Spell Sentence grammar is universal — what changes between a spell, a sword strike, and a social argument is the cable, not the signal.

Document 4 specifies the combat petal: the talent trees that unlock physical transmission dialects. Each tree opens grammatical constructions the body can produce — effects, additives, Construction Pool parameters — that were inaccessible before training. A character without the Finesse Style Crux cannot compose precision expressions through their body. After training, the channel opens.

This document covers the 23 confirmed combat petal trees across four categories.

-----
# <a name="_heading=h.ixm5gz1aaund"></a>**PART I: THE TRANSMISSION FRAMEWORK**
## <a name="_heading=h.gc9xt1cjtc0s"></a>**Three Transmission Media**
The Spell Sentence grammar produces identical effects regardless of medium. What differs is the cable:

|Medium|Scope|FP Source|Constraints|
| :- | :- | :- | :- |
|**Æther** (direct magical)|Micro (internal pattern) + Macro (environmental interface)|Metaphysical FP (ÆP)|Formal composition, isotope access, Mastery cap|
|**Body** (physical technique)|Middle — the body IS the cable|Physical FP (Stamina)|Body’s physical properties, weapon material, trained reflexes|
|**Item** (material substrate)|Middle — the item IS the cable|Item’s charge pool (drip recharge)|Material properties, Formation Stage, housing architecture|

Talents specify what the body-as-cable can transmit. Without training, the body can only produce the most basic expressions: raw Damage through an unrefined strike, basic Movement through walking. Training opens channels for complex compositions — linked effects, additive-modified expressions, expanded Construction Pool access.
## <a name="_heading=h.q5ceqg2iacvn"></a>**What a Talent Actually Does**
A talent is NOT a pre-built technique. It is a TRANSMISSION PARAMETER UNLOCK.

|Talent Component|What It Unlocks|
| :- | :- |
|**Crux talent**|Opens the channel — you can now transmit on this frequency|
|**Novice progression**|Bandwidth increases — more effects composable through this channel|
|**Journeyman progression**|Signal fidelity increases — higher ESL, more CP access, cleaner output|
|**Master progression**|Full channel capacity — complex linked compositions, maximum ESL, full Additive access|

Players don’t select from a menu of techniques. They compose physical expressions within the grammatical constraints their talents have unlocked, the same way a spellcaster composes rotes within their available grammar.
## <a name="_heading=h.nxv2dujbgsmo"></a>**The Physical Expression Formula**
A physical technique decomposes identically to a spell:

**Physical Expression = Effect + Isotope (weapon/body material) + Construction Pool (geometry) + Additives/Subtractives**

The key differences from magical composition:

|Parameter|Magical (Tier 3)|Physical (Tier 1)|
| :- | :- | :- |
|Effect selection|Any effect the caster has dots in|Only effects unlocked by talent trees|
|Isotope|External materials, foci|Weapon material or body composition|
|CP geometry|Full CP access based on formula|Restricted by talent tier — starts at melee/touch/instant, expands with training|
|ESL cap|min(Effect Tier, Isotope Tier, Mastery)|Skill ranks + relevant talent tier|
|FP source|Metaphysical FP|Physical FP|
|Composition method|Conscious, deliberate|Trained reflex, unconscious|
## <a name="_heading=h.of7upay5ack1"></a>**Tempo Costs by Commitment**
Physical expressions cost tempo based on complexity, not power:

|Commitment|Tempo|What It Means|
| :- | :- | :- |
|Light (1)|Simple, single-effect expression|A basic strike, a quick parry, a short movement|
|Standard (2)|Moderate composition — linked effects or moderate CP|A Power Attack (Damage + Enhanced Trait linked), a precise shot, a combat maneuver|
|Heavy (3)|Complex composition — multiple links, high CP, or cross-pillar|A Stunning Fist (Damage + Affliction linked), a Whirlwind Attack (Damage + Burst geometry), a Spellstrike|

-----
# <a name="_heading=h.s4evi7mj3zwd"></a>**PART II: THE CRUX & DROPLET MODEL**
## <a name="_heading=h.mdtptrvqrlkv"></a>**Three-Tier Hierarchy**
All combat talents are organized into three tiers representing progression from basic competency to specialized mastery:

**Tier 1 — Foundational Talents.** The basic building blocks. Not tied to any specific Precept. A character’s initial selections almost always come from this group. These are the Crux talents that open channels.

**Tier 2 — Core Precept Talents (Droplets).** Specialized abilities within a specific tree that flow into and enhance the Crux. Each Droplet requires its parent Crux and often has additional prerequisites (Mastery rating, skill ranks, or prior Droplet mastery).

**Tier 3 — General Martial Talents.** Broad category of talents not tied to a specific Crux but may have Foundational prerequisites. Any character can purchase these to customize their build. Cross-tree talents live here.
## <a name="_heading=h.f0pydurysyn4"></a>**The Prerequisite Chaining Discount**
The XP cost of a talent is reduced for each prerequisite in its direct chain. This rewards deep investment in a single tree:

•            The Crux talent is “Level 0” — full cost

•            First Droplet in the chain — full cost (no discount)

•            Second Droplet — -1 XP discount

•            Third Droplet — -2 XP discount

•            And so on

This produces diminishing costs for deep specialization. A character who invests heavily in a single tree pays less per talent the deeper they go, incentivizing commitment over breadth.
## <a name="_heading=h.9r5s31glc8zk"></a>**The Unique Talent Rule**
Talents can only be taken once unless their description explicitly states otherwise. Talents marked “can be taken multiple times” (like Expanded Reserves) are explicitly noted.
## <a name="_heading=h.owbavk9kygyo"></a>**Stances as a Talent Subtype Tag**
A Stance is not its own tree. It is a TAG applied to specific talents within other trees. A talent tagged [Stance] represents a sustained combat posture that modifies the character’s expression profile while active. Only one Stance can be active at a time. Switching Stances costs 1 tempo (Light action).

Rage (within the Durability tree) is a [Stance]. Defensive Stance (within Agile Defense) is a [Stance]. Phalanx Formation (within Way of the Phalanx) is a [Stance].

-----
# <a name="_heading=h.fp800y6w57m"></a>**PART III: FOUNDATIONAL STYLES (8 TREES)**
These are the core disciplines that form the bedrock of martial training. Each defines the fundamental “how” of a character’s physical combat expression.

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.6i0wgde65h5d"></a>**1. Two-Handed Style**
**Philosophy:** Overwhelming force through a single, heavy weapon. Maximum energy per strike. The embodiment of “I hit it harder.”

**Crux Talent:** Power Attack **Core Mechanic:** Take a scaling penalty to attack rolls to gain a bonus to damage. This IS a Damage + Weaken (self, accuracy) Subtractive composition — you’re trading signal precision for signal amplitude.

**Transmission Specifications:**

|Tier|Effects Unlocked|Additives Unlocked|CP Access|
| :- | :- | :- | :- |
|**Novice**|Damage (amplified), Enhanced Trait (Might, self), Move Object (Shove)|Overwhelming (-4, all-or-nothing)|Melee, Individual, Instantaneous|
|**Journeyman**|Damage (area — Cleave geometry), Affliction (knockdown through impact)|Multiattack (Cleave — arcing strike hits adjacent), Split|Melee, Burst (small — weapon reach arc)|
|**Master**|Damage (maximized — Vital Strike), Nullify (Sunder — destroying equipment/expressions through force)|Penetrating (ignoring Soak partially)|Melee, Individual (single target, maximum force)|

**Representative Decompositions:**

|Technique|Spell Sentence Decomposition|
| :- | :- |
|Power Attack|Damage + Subtractive: Reduced Accuracy. Amplitude increases proportionally to accuracy penalty.|
|Cleave|Damage + Burst (small, weapon arc). Subtractive: Reduced Accuracy. Adjacent targets in arc take overflow damage.|
|Vital Strike|Damage at maximum ESL. Single target, single strike. No Multiattack — all energy into one expression.|
|Sunder|Nullify targeting item Pattern. Attack vs item’s Physical Composite Defense. Success = item condition track damage.|
|Shove|Move Object (forced). Might-based attack vs target’s Guard Defense. Success = target displaced.|

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.x8xytf9voxi5"></a>**2. Finesse Style**
**Philosophy:** Precision, speed, and exploiting openings. Accuracy over power. The needle over the hammer.

**Crux Talent:** Weapon Finesse **Core Mechanic:** Use Finesse attribute instead of Might for attack rolls with appropriate weapons. This changes the transmission’s governing attribute — the signal routes through agility rather than force.

**Transmission Specifications:**

|Tier|Effects Unlocked|Additives Unlocked|CP Access|
| :- | :- | :- | :- |
|**Novice**|Damage (precision-routed), Protection (Parry — reactive defense), Illusion (Feint — false intent)|Triggered (+2, reactive parry)|Melee, Individual, Instantaneous|
|**Journeyman**|Damage + Protection linked (Parry-Riposte), Weaken (exploiting gaps in defense), Senses (reading opponent’s stance)|Selective (choosing which target in reach)|Melee, Individual|
|**Master**|Damage (bypassing Soak through precision — Precision Damage integration), Affliction (targeted strikes to specific anatomy)|Penetrating (precision bypass), Alternate Resistance (targeting unexpected defense axis)|Melee, Individual (surgical precision)|

**Representative Decompositions:**

|Technique|Spell Sentence Decomposition|
| :- | :- |
|Weapon Finesse|Damage routed through Finesse attribute rather than Might. Same effect, different governing stat.|
|Parry|Protection + Triggered (+2). Reactive defense using weapon. Costs FP, not tempo.|
|Parry-Riposte|Protection + Damage linked. Triggered. Reactive parry immediately followed by counter-strike. Single FP expenditure for both.|
|Feint|Illusion (single sense — false intent) targeting Social Dodge. Success strips target’s Physical Dodge for next attack. Cross-pillar expression.|

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.7vug3gtdj67a"></a>**3. Two-Weapon Style**
**Philosophy:** Dual wielding. Two transmission cables operating simultaneously. Increased output at the cost of individual signal strength.

**Crux Talent:** Two-Weapon Fighting **Core Mechanic:** Reduce penalties for attacking with two weapons. This is a Multiattack additive applied to the body’s dual-channel transmission.

**Transmission Specifications:**

|Tier|Effects Unlocked|Additives Unlocked|CP Access|
| :- | :- | :- | :- |
|**Novice**|Damage (dual delivery), Protection (off-hand defensive parry)|Multiattack (reduced penalty dual strikes)|Melee, Individual × 2 channels|
|**Journeyman**|Damage (improved off-hand — full Finesse routing), Affliction (dual condition application)|Chain (off-hand strike chains to second target)|Melee, Individual × 2, potential 2 targets|
|**Master**|Damage (flurry — multiple strikes per hand per round), Weaken (relentless pressure degrading defense)|Multiattack (full flurry), Split (damage distributed across multiple targets)|Melee, Burst (personal reach — everything in range)|

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.vwetc1yurq17"></a>**4. Ranged Style**
**Philosophy:** Control from distance. Projecting expressions beyond melee through projectile transmission — arrows, bolts, bullets.

**Crux Talent:** Point-Blank Shot **Core Mechanic:** Bonus to attack and damage at close range. The foundation for all ranged expression — you must learn to transmit at close range before extending distance.

**Transmission Specifications:**

|Tier|Effects Unlocked|Additives Unlocked|CP Access|
| :- | :- | :- | :- |
|**Novice**|Damage (ranged delivery), Senses (spotting — ranged perception advantage)|—|Close-Medium range, Individual, Instantaneous|
|**Journeyman**|Damage (Deadly Aim — ranged Power Attack equivalent), Affliction (pinning shots — movement denial)|Selective (Precise Shot — negating cover/crowd penalties), Multiattack (Rapid Shot — volume fire)|Medium-Long range, Individual|
|**Master**|Damage (extreme range precision), Nullify (disabling shots — targeting equipment), Affliction (called shots — targeting specific anatomy at range)|Penetrating (armor-piercing), Indirect (ricochet/arcing shots)|Long-Visual Sight range, Individual|

**Sub-dialect: Firearms.** Setting B Kan-tech firearms use the same Ranged Style tree but with different isotope profiles (weapon material determines damage type) and different reload mechanics (Kan-tech charge cells vs physical ammunition). The transmission specifications are identical — the weapon is a different cable carrying the same signal.

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.uckdg15b8f3j"></a>**5. Unarmed Style**
**Philosophy:** The body IS the weapon. No external cable needed. Pure physical transmission through flesh, bone, and trained reflex.

**Crux Talent:** Trained Fists **Core Mechanic:** Unarmed strikes become viable, non-provoking, lethal combat options. The body’s natural transmission capacity is unlocked for Damage delivery.

**Transmission Specifications:**

|Tier|Effects Unlocked|Additives Unlocked|CP Access|
| :- | :- | :- | :- |
|**Novice**|Damage (unarmed — body’s own isotope composition), Movement (enhanced mobility — flips, rolls, rapid footwork)|—|Melee (touch), Individual|
|**Journeyman**|Damage (Flurry of Blows — rapid multi-strike), Affliction (Stunning Fist — BER 4 Stunned through nerve strike), Enhanced Trait (self — body hardening)|Multiattack (Flurry), Alternate Resistance (nerve strikes targeting Mental defense)|Melee (touch), Individual × Flurry count|
|**Master**|Damage (lethal unarmed at weapon-equivalent ESL), Transform (minor — body modification mid-combat: iron body, stone fist), Immunity (self — physical damage resistance through body conditioning)|Penetrating (ki-enhanced strikes ignoring Soak), Overwhelming|Melee (touch), extending to Close range through projected ki/æther|

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.slvi6b24puns"></a>**6. Shield Combat**
**Philosophy:** The shield as an active tool — offense, defense, and ally protection simultaneously. Dual-purpose transmission: defensive AND offensive through the same implement.

**Crux Talent:** Improved Shield Bash **Core Mechanic:** Attack with shield without losing defensive bonus. The shield transmits Protection AND Damage simultaneously rather than choosing one.

**Transmission Specifications:**

|Tier|Effects Unlocked|Additives Unlocked|CP Access|
| :- | :- | :- | :- |
|**Novice**|Damage (shield bash), Protection (passive shield defense + active blocking)|Triggered (reactive block — Missile Shield)|Melee, Individual|
|**Journeyman**|Protection (Saving Shield — extending defense to adjacent ally), Damage + Protection linked (bash-and-block), Move Object (shield shove)|Selective (choosing which ally to protect), Affects Others (+2, extending Protection)|Melee, Individual + 1 adjacent ally|
|**Master**|Protection (Shield Wall — group defense in formation), Damage (Bashing Finish — free attack on critical), Nullify (shield absorbing magical effects)|Burst (small — formation coverage), Triggered (reactive counter on any blocked attack)|Melee, Burst (small — adjacent squares)|

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.jbble010mk4f"></a>**7. Mounted Combat**
**Philosophy:** Rider and mount as unified organism. Two bodies, one expression. The mount provides Movement and mass; the rider provides direction and technique.

**Crux Talent:** Mounted Defender **Core Mechanic:** Use your skill to protect your mount from harm. The rider’s defensive training extends to cover the mount’s vulnerabilities.

**Transmission Specifications:**

|Tier|Effects Unlocked|Additives Unlocked|CP Access|
| :- | :- | :- | :- |
|**Novice**|Movement (mount-provided — enhanced speed, terrain crossing), Protection (rider defending mount), Damage (mounted melee with momentum bonus)|—|Melee (from mount height), Medium range (mounted archery)|
|**Journeyman**|Damage (Ride-By Attack — hit-and-run charge), Damage (Spirited Charge — devastating impact), Movement (rapid repositioning)|Chain (charge through multiple targets)|Extended melee reach (charge line)|
|**Master**|Damage (Mounted Archery — precision fire from moving platform), Protection (mount as living shield), Enhanced Trait (mount — pushing mount beyond limits)|Multiattack (multiple targets during charge pass), Selective (choosing which targets in charge path)|Charge line, Long range (mounted archery)|

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.8gi4j8vq96dc"></a>**8. Reach Control**
**Philosophy:** Area denial through extended reach. Controlling space — who enters, who exits, who passes through your threatened zone. The polearm specialist, the whip master, the zone controller.

**Crux Talent:** Reach Weapon Training **Core Mechanic:** Extended threatened area. Your transmission range expands beyond normal melee, creating a zone of control around your position.

**Transmission Specifications:**

|Tier|Effects Unlocked|Additives Unlocked|CP Access|
| :- | :- | :- | :- |
|**Novice**|Damage (at extended range — 10ft reach), Affliction (Trip — BER 2 Prone through leverage at distance)|Triggered (Attack of Opportunity — reactive strikes against movement through zone)|Melee (extended — 10ft), Individual|
|**Journeyman**|Damage (zone denial — everything entering reach takes reactive damage), Move Object (push/pull at distance — hooking weapons), Weaken (reach-based disarm)|Selective (choosing which targets trigger reactions), Burst (emanation — threatened zone as area)|Melee (extended), Emanation (threatened zone)|
|**Master**|Damage + Movement linked (Whirlwind Attack — spinning strike hitting all in reach), Protection (lockdown — preventing all movement through zone), Affliction (pinning at distance)|Multiattack (Whirlwind), Burst (medium — full reach coverage)|Melee (extended), Burst (reach radius)|

-----
# <a name="_heading=h.tf65di28esxw"></a>**PART IV: DEFENSIVE PHILOSOPHIES (3 TREES)**
These trees define HOW a character absorbs, avoids, or endures damage. They are primarily Protection-focused but each has a distinct transmission philosophy.

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.94whfddpt606"></a>**9. Durability**
**Philosophy:** Endurance through toughness. Absorb the hit and keep fighting. The anvil, not the dodge.

**Crux Talent:** Armor Training **Core Mechanic:** Mitigate the downsides of heavy armor. The body is trained to operate within heavy transmission dampening (armor penalty reduction) while maximizing the armor’s Protection expression.

**Transmission Specifications:**

|Tier|Effects Unlocked|Additives Unlocked|CP Access|
| :- | :- | :- | :- |
|**Novice**|Protection (armor optimization — increased Soak, reduced penalties), Enhanced Trait (Vigor — endurance in sustained combat)|—|Self, Concentration (sustained stance)|
|**Journeyman**|Protection (Stalwart — converting Dodge into DR), Healing (self — second wind, recovering HP mid-combat through sheer endurance), Immunity (self, partial — resistance to specific conditions)|Persistent (Protection doesn’t drop when you’re hit)|Self|
|**Master**|Protection (Armor Mastery — critical hit immunity), Immunity (self — condition resistance through pure endurance), Nullify (Armor absorbing magical effects)|—|Self|

**The Rage [Stance]:** Rage lives within Durability as a [Stance] tag talent. Activating Rage grants Enhanced Trait (Might, self) + Weaken (self, mental attributes) + Protection (temporary HP/DR) as a linked expression sustained through Physical FP drain. It’s not its own tree — it’s a Durability Stance that trades mental signal for physical amplification.

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.1fflvdhhn6xx"></a>**10. Agile Defense**
**Philosophy:** Don’t be where the attack lands. Speed, awareness, reflexes. The mosquito, not the anvil.

**Crux Talent:** Uncanny Dodge **Core Mechanic:** Never caught flat-footed. Your defensive transmission is ALWAYS active — the channel never closes.

**Transmission Specifications:**

|Tier|Effects Unlocked|Additives Unlocked|CP Access|
| :- | :- | :- | :- |
|**Novice**|Protection (Dodge-based — reflexive avoidance), Movement (Mobility — moving through threatened areas safely), Senses (danger awareness — never surprised)|Triggered (reactive dodge)|Self, Instantaneous|
|**Journeyman**|Protection (Evasion — area effects deal no damage on successful save), Movement (enhanced repositioning), Illusion (self-concealment through speed)|—|Self|
|**Master**|Protection (Improved Uncanny Dodge — immune to flanking), Illusion (blinding speed — attacks seem to miss impossibly), Movement (dimensional step — short-range micro-teleportation through Palimpsest skim)|Selective (choosing which attacks to avoid)|Self, Close range (dimensional step)|

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.y8074s8ogubc"></a>**11. Way of the Phalanx**
**Philosophy:** Formation defense. Group Protection expression. The shield wall, the firing line, the organized unit. Individual capability sacrificed for collective resilience.

**Crux Talent:** Shield Wall **Core Mechanic:** When adjacent to an ally with a shield, both gain enhanced defensive bonuses. The individual’s Protection expression links to adjacent allies, creating a networked defense.

**Transmission Specifications:**

|Tier|Effects Unlocked|Additives Unlocked|CP Access|
| :- | :- | :- | :- |
|**Novice**|Protection (formation — shared defense bonuses with adjacent shield-bearers), Damage (coordinated strike — bonus when flanking with formation ally)|Affects Others (+2, formation Protection), Selective (excluding allies from own AoE)|Melee, Burst (small — formation footprint)|
|**Journeyman**|Protection (Mobile Bulwark — total cover from one direction), Enhanced Trait (allies — formation morale), Senses (formation awareness — shared perception across the line)|Burst (medium — larger formation), Persistent (formation bonuses don’t drop when one member is hit)|Melee, Burst (formation), duration: Concentration|
|**Master**|Protection (Unbreakable Line — formation members share HP damage, distributing hits across the unit), Healing (formation recovery — shared endurance), Damage (Phalanx Charge — coordinated movement and strike)|Burst (large — full unit), Chain (damage distribution across formation)|Formation footprint, Concentration (sustained)|

-----
# <a name="_heading=h.rlb2eu69j3mu"></a>**PART V: TACTICAL/GENERAL TREES (7 TREES)**
These trees provide combat augmentation available to any character regardless of Foundational Style. They layer on top of the base transmission.

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.ycqp7lqx80dp"></a>**12. Intimidation Style**
**Philosophy:** Weaponized fear. Cross-pillar expression — Physical presence producing Social/Mental effects. The body as a social weapon.

**Crux Talent:** Dazzling Display **Core Mechanic:** Show of force with a weapon to intimidate multiple enemies simultaneously. This IS a cross-pillar Damage + Affliction expression: Physical display → Mental/Social condition.

**Transmission Specifications:**

|Tier|Effects Unlocked|Additives Unlocked|CP Access|
| :- | :- | :- | :- |
|**Novice**|Affliction (Frightened — BER 3 targeting Mental Composite Defense), Damage (Social — Reputation damage through physical threat)|Alternate Resistance (Physical → Mental/Social), Burst (small — all who witness)|Close range (visual), Burst (witnesses)|
|**Journeyman**|Affliction (Shaken sustained — persistent fear aura), Weaken (Social Dodge strip — targets too frightened to dodge social attacks), Mind Link (Goad — forcing target to attack you)|Persistent (fear aura sustains), Selective (choosing which targets are affected)|Close range, Burst (medium)|
|**Master**|Affliction (Panicked — BER 4+, forced rout), Damage (Mental — psychological damage through pure terror), Immunity (self — fear resistance through practiced menace)|Burst (large — battlefield-wide intimidation), Overwhelming|Visual Sight range, Burst (all witnesses)|

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.q8zxi17le94o"></a>**13. Combat Maneuvers**
**Philosophy:** Tactical manipulation — controlling the opponent’s position, equipment, and capabilities through technique rather than damage. Trip, Grapple, Disarm, Sunder, Shove.

**Crux Talent:** Combat Maneuver Training **Core Mechanic:** Access to the five core maneuvers without provoking reactions. Each maneuver is a specific effect composition.

**Maneuver Decompositions:**

|Maneuver|Effect Composition|Attack vs. Defense|
| :- | :- | :- |
|**Trip**|Affliction (Prone, BER 2)|Might/Finesse vs Guard|
|**Grapple**|Affliction (Restrained, BER 4). Sustained. Escalates to Pinned on critical.|Might vs Guard|
|**Disarm**|Move Object (targeting held item)|Finesse vs Guard|
|**Sunder**|Nullify (targeting item Pattern — item condition track damage)|Might vs item Physical Composite Defense|
|**Shove**|Move Object (forced displacement) + Movement (forced)|Might vs Guard|

**Progression:** Novice grants basic access. Journeyman grants improved versions (no reaction provoked, bonus to checks). Master grants Greater versions (immediate follow-up actions, enhanced effects on critical success).

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.fckx51p41lyo"></a>**14. Critical Specialization**
**Philosophy:** Exploiting the Palimpsest’s probability collapse for maximum effect. Two branches representing different approaches to critical expression.

**Precision Branch:** Extend the critical window. Keen-style threat range expansion. For surgical fighters who create more critical opportunities through accuracy.

**Brutal Branch:** Amplify the critical effect. For heavy hitters who make each critical devastating. Enhanced isotope critical expressions, additional condition track movement, expanded secondary effects.

Both branches modify the critical system from Document 2 Part 3 — they don’t replace it, they enhance specific parameters within it.

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.v602bytq8a0o"></a>**15. Hunter/Mark System (Vow of Enmity)**
**Philosophy:** Single-target focus. Consolidated from Favored Enemy, Smite, Challenge, and Studied Target into a unified marking system.

**Crux Talent:** Mark Target **Core Mechanic:** Designate a single enemy. Gain scaling bonuses against that target. Penalties against all other targets. This is Enhanced Trait (self, against marked target) + Weaken (self, against all others) — the trade-off between focus and flexibility.

**Transmission Specifications:** Novice grants basic marking (minor bonus). Journeyman grants sustained mark with enhanced bonuses. Master grants mark-on-kill chain (defeating marked target immediately transfers mark to next target) and cross-pillar mark application (knowledge of the marked target grants Social and Mental advantages).

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.rdpt6bsmg8d1"></a>**16. Commander/Tactician**
**Philosophy:** Force multiplication through leadership. Your expressions benefit your allies’ expressions. The officer, the warlord, the battlefield conductor.

**Crux Talent:** Aura of Command **Core Mechanic:** Allies within your command radius gain bonuses. This is Enhanced Trait (others) + Protection (others) transmitted through Social/Mental pillar but benefiting Physical performance. Cross-pillar expression.

**Transmission Specifications:** Novice grants basic command aura (small radius, minor bonuses). Journeyman grants tactical coordination (allies can use your Initiative for specific maneuvers, Teamwork talents activate). Master grants battlefield control (large radius, significant bonuses, ability to grant extra actions to allies through command).

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.ix6qz8e62aed"></a>**17. Precision Damage**
**Philosophy:** Finding the weak point and exploiting it for maximum effect. Bypassing defenses through accuracy rather than force.

**Crux Talent:** Exploit Opening **Core Mechanic:** When a target is denied their Dodge defense (flat-footed, flanked, feinted), your Damage expression bypasses their Soak partially or fully. This is Damage + Penetrating additive, conditional on the target’s defensive state.

**Progression:** Novice grants basic Precision Damage dice. Journeyman grants increased dice and the ability to apply precision to ranged attacks. Master grants precision against targets normally immune (constructs, elementals — through anatomical/structural knowledge).

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.dmrjfnsruufi"></a>**18. Finisher**
**Philosophy:** The killing blow. Techniques designed to end a fight decisively when the opponent is already weakened.

**Crux Talent:** Finishing Strike **Core Mechanic:** When a target is at a specific condition track state or below a HP threshold, your Damage expression gains massive amplification. This is Damage + Enhanced Trait (self, conditional) — the burst of finishing energy when the prey is wounded.

**Progression:** Novice grants bonus damage against wounded targets (below half HP). Journeyman grants coup de grace improvements and execution mechanics. Master grants the ability to force condition track movement on wounded targets through a single devastating strike.

-----
# <a name="_heading=h.e3qnihm5b8by"></a>**PART VI: ESOTERIC/RESOURCE TREES (5 TREES)**
These trees represent specialized combat approaches that draw on exotic resources — mutations, soul-energy, construct companions, or adaptive flexibility.

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.21kynlwdrz7c"></a>**19. Forge Construct (Sōmaturgy — External)**
**Philosophy:** Creating a separate entity to fight alongside you. The construct companion path.

**Crux Talent:** Forge Construct **Core Mechanic:** Summon an æther-construct. Choose a base chassis (Biped, Quadruped, etc.). The construct uses the same character engine as players (Spell = Item = Creature). ESL caps the Creature Mastery Level.

**Transmission:** You invest Physical and Metaphysical FP into the construct’s operation. The construct’s Evolution Pool (customization budget) scales with your Mastery. The construct acts on its own initiative but follows your commands through Mind Link.

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.o4khq8c855p3"></a>**20. Shape Soulmeld (Sōmaturgy — Internal)**
**Philosophy:** Manifesting your soul-energy as semi-permanent armor, weapons, and tools worn on the body. Akashic magic adapted as physical technique.

**Crux Talent:** Shape Veil **Core Mechanic:** Shape æther into a “Veil” occupying a specific body slot (hands, feet, head, etc.). Invest Stamina/Æther into the Veil to empower it. More investment = more powerful expression.

**Transmission:** Each Veil IS a sustained expression occupying a body slot. The investiture mechanic is a sustained-duration Protection/Enhanced Trait/Damage expression powered by continuous FP drain. Reallocating investment is a Light action (1 tempo) — shifting FP between Veils mid-combat is a tactical decision.

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.rdmbmxii4x7n"></a>**21. Mutagenic Formula**
**Philosophy:** Controlled self-transformation through alchemical science. Setting B’s answer to wild mutation — not random adaptation but engineered enhancement.

**Crux Talent:** Mutagen **Core Mechanic:** Create and consume a mutagen that grants Enhanced Trait (Physical attribute) + Weaken (Mental attribute). A controlled Transform expression delivered through alchemical substrate rather than magical or physical medium.

**Transmission:** The mutagen IS an item-medium expression consumed by the body. Feral Mutagen (claws/bite) is Transform (minor, Tier 1) stacked on the base Enhanced Trait. The Mutagenic Formula tree expands what Transform compositions the mutagen can carry.

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.ssnosujcfxl7"></a>**22. Martial Flexibility (Praxis Collegium Signature)**
**Philosophy:** The ultimate generalist. Temporarily gaining access to talent transmissions you don’t permanently possess. The Scholar’s Gambit embodied.

**Crux Talent:** Martial Flexibility **Core Mechanic:** As a Standard action, temporarily gain access to a talent you meet prerequisites for but don’t possess. The talent remains active for a limited duration. This is Variable (bounded) applied to the talent system itself — you’re replicating a transmission parameter you’ve studied but haven’t permanently trained.

**Transmission:** The cost is tempo (Standard action to switch) plus FP (sustained drain while the borrowed talent is active). You cannot exceed your Mastery cap for the borrowed talent’s ESL. The Praxis Collegium’s overt Way of the Scholar’s Gambit assembles from this tree — the warrior who always has the right tool for the right moment.

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.ohs071eymtk4"></a>**23. Systemic Catalyst**
**Philosophy:** Force multiplication through environmental leverage. Targeting natural systems to produce disproportionate effects. Documented fully in Document 2 Part 3 (Part VIII).

**Crux Talent:** Systemic Catalyst **Core Mechanic:** Identify a leverage point in a natural system and apply a standard effect to produce amplified consequences. Multiplier scales with system scale (Local ×2 through Territorial ×500).

**Transmission:** The expression IS a standard Damage/Nullify/Transform effect delivered at Individual target. The Catalyst talent multiplies the consequence through the natural system rather than through increased ESL. Requires Survival ranks (natural systems), Artifice ranks (engineered systems), or equivalent Knowledge-petal investment to identify leverage points.

-----
# <a name="_heading=h.e2o902zi4tw"></a>**PART VII: THE 18 ÆTHERFORGED PRECEPTS AS ASSEMBLY BLUEPRINTS**
## <a name="_heading=h.2p63wbtbnf5s"></a>**Precepts Are Not Trees**
The 18 Ætherforged Precepts (nine colleges, each with an Overt Way and a Covert Way) are NOT additional talent trees. They are ASSEMBLY BLUEPRINTS — recommended combinations of the trees from Parts III-VI that produce a specific martial philosophy.

A player doesn’t buy the “Way of the Still Verse.” They buy the specific Foundational Styles, Defensive Philosophies, and Tactical trees that the Way of the Still Verse prescribes. The Precept is the blueprint; the trees are the components.
## <a name="_heading=h.vcq0subrsmul"></a>**Assembly Structure**
Each Precept specifies:

|Component|Description|
| :- | :- |
|**Crux Talents (1-2)**|The mandatory entry points. Without these, you’re not practicing this Way.|
|**Supporting Trees (9-10)**|The recommended investment path. These trees compose the Precept’s characteristic expression profile.|
|**Signature Compositions**|The linked expressions that define the Way’s identity — the specific physical sentences this dialect produces.|
## <a name="_heading=h.ocfyqbuo3djs"></a>**Representative Precepts**
### <a name="_heading=h.l792gwscazp"></a>**Mortis Ævum — Overt: The Way of the Still Verse**
**Philosophy:** Methodical, surgical deconstruction. The anatomist’s blade.

**Assembly:** Crux: Finesse Style, Precision Damage. Supporting: Two-Weapon Style (paired surgical instruments), Agile Defense, Combat Maneuvers (Disarm), Critical Specialization (Precision Branch), Medicine-informed anatomical targeting.

**Signature Composition:** Damage + Affliction linked, delivered through Finesse with Precision Damage conditional and Penetrating additive. The Still Verse practitioner’s strike IS a surgical operation — they know exactly where to cut because Medicine informed their targeting, and Finesse routes the expression through precision rather than force.
### <a name="_heading=h.yu8td7b6049k"></a>**Stormpeak — Overt: The Way of the Phalanx**
**Philosophy:** Disciplined, unyielding line-holding. Order as strategy.

**Assembly:** Crux: Shield Combat, Durability (Armor Training). Supporting: Way of the Phalanx, Commander (Aura of Command), Intimidation Style, Combat Maneuvers (Shove), Mounted Combat, Finesse Style (for defense).

**Signature Composition:** Protection (formation, Burst) + Enhanced Trait (allies) + Damage (coordinated strike). The Phalanx warrior’s expression is SHARED — their Protection extends to adjacent allies, their Enhanced Trait aura buffs the formation, and their Damage expression synchronizes with the unit’s collective strike.
### <a name="_heading=h.dmuayfm5ohhv"></a>**Stormpeak — Covert: The Way of the Vanguard**
**Philosophy:** Brutal shock trooper. Break the line through overwhelming force.

**Assembly:** Crux: Two-Handed Style, Durability (Rage [Stance]). Supporting: Combat Maneuvers (Sunder), Critical Specialization (Brutal Branch), Intimidation Style, Finisher, Mounted Combat (Charge Focus).

**Signature Composition:** Damage (amplified) + Enhanced Trait (self, Rage) + Affliction (fear aura) linked. The Vanguard’s expression is a WALL OF FORCE — maximum amplitude, intimidating presence, and the ability to break both equipment (Sunder) and morale (Intimidation) simultaneously.
### <a name="_heading=h.5ettrpgp0sc8"></a>**Deepguard — Overt: The Way of the Patient Snare**
**Philosophy:** Master bodyguard. Defensive controller. Neutralize with minimal force.

**Assembly:** Crux: Combat Maneuvers (Control). Supporting: Finesse Style, Agile Defense, Shield Combat, Durability, Commander (Protector Auras).

**Signature Composition:** Affliction (Restrained/Disarmed/Prone) + Protection (ally-directed). The Patient Snare practitioner’s expression is CONTROL — they impose conditions rather than deal damage, and their Protection expressions are directed outward to allies rather than inward to self.
### <a name="_heading=h.2ic1hlq16ukz"></a>**Praxis Collegium — Overt: The Way of the Scholar’s Gambit**
**Philosophy:** Universal adaptability. The right tool for every situation.

**Assembly:** Crux: Martial Flexibility, Ranged Style (Firearms). Supporting: Finesse Style, Two-Weapon Style (blade/firearm combo), Agile Defense, Durability, Commander (Tactician).

**Signature Composition:** Variable (bounded, through Martial Flexibility) + any Foundational Style’s expressions as needed. The Scholar’s Gambit practitioner’s identity IS their flexibility — they don’t have a signature strike because they can compose ANY strike the situation demands.

-----
*The remaining 13 Precept assemblies follow the same pattern. Each college’s Overt and Covert Ways are constructed by combining specific trees from the combat petal. The trees are the vocabulary. The Precepts are the dialects. The character is the speaker.*

-----
*End of Document 4. This document integrates with Documents 1-3 through the shared Spell Sentence grammar, the unified FP economy, the tempo model, and the 23+Variable effect vocabulary. Each tree’s transmission specifications can be verified against the Skill × Effect Compatibility Matrix. Future documents will address the Knowledge petal (utility/crafting trees), the Philosophy petal (magical trees from the Book of Shadows), and the Social petal (performance/influence/investigation trees).*
# <a name="_heading=h.hqwgu7gmipa9"></a>**THE ÆTHERIUM CODEX — GENERAL & UTILITY TALENT TREES**
## <a name="_heading=h.d7bi6wqgz2i2"></a>**Document 4b: Non-Combat Transmission Specifications**
### <a name="_heading=h.7ggqde3qpl0m"></a>**Canonical Draft — April 16, 2026**
-----
## <a name="_heading=h.nc6zjkqi5jxo"></a>**PREAMBLE**
Document 4 specified the combat petal — 23 trees defining martial physical expression. Document 4b covers the general and utility trees that operate outside or alongside combat. These trees unlock transmission channels for investigation, crafting, social maneuvering, stealth, perception, performance, survival, and defensive resilience.

These trees use the same transmission framework. Each Crux opens a channel. Each Droplet expands bandwidth. The Spell Sentence grammar is unchanged — what differs is the application domain.

Organization follows the four skill pillars. Trees that span multiple skills are listed under their primary skill.

-----
# <a name="_heading=h.xbwsogevbhk0"></a>**PART I: PHYSICAL PILLAR UTILITY TREES**
## <a name="_heading=h.7gezww5tw1i4"></a>**Acrobatics (Athletics)**
**Crux:** Graceful Motion — add Mastery Rating to jumping, balancing, and tumbling checks.

**Channel Opens:** Movement (enhanced — flips, rolls, tumbling), Protection (reactive — Fortuitous Contortion negates criticals through body positioning), Illusion (self — appearing to be elsewhere through acrobatic misdirection).

|Talent|Effect Decomposition|
| :- | :- |
|Body Shield Fall|Protection (reactive, Triggered) — using another creature to absorb fall damage. Damage + Split.|
|Careful Movement|Movement + Protection linked — move through threatened areas without triggering reactions. First 10 feet of threatened movement is safe.|
|Eel’s Grace|Protection (substitute attribute) — use Finesse instead of Might against Grapple. Re-routes the defensive transmission through a different governing stat.|
|Fortuitous Contortion|Protection (reactive, costs Stamina) — negate critical hit. Athletics check vs attack roll. The body twists to convert a devastating hit into a normal one.|
|Unfettered Freedom|Protection (passive) — add Mastery to defense against entangle/paralyze/impede effects. Sustained resistance to Movement-denying Afflictions.|

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.vngcuouspi62"></a>**Mobility (Athletics)**
**Crux:** Fleet of Foot — base land speed increased by 5 feet, scaling with Mastery.

**Channel Opens:** Movement (enhanced speed, terrain navigation), Protection (movement-based defense).

|Talent|Effect Decomposition|
| :- | :- |
|Fleet of Foot|Movement + Enhanced Trait (self, speed). Permanent transmission upgrade — base Movement expression is stronger.|
|Careful Movement|Movement through threatened areas without provoking. Protection through positioning rather than armor.|
|Nimble Moves|Movement ignoring first 5 feet of difficult terrain. Nullify (environmental, partial) — negating terrain-based Movement penalties.|

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.7otihw8lpbis"></a>**Endurance (Athletics)**
**Crux:** Resilient Body — add Mastery to checks against fatigue, exhaustion, and harsh environments.

**Channel Opens:** Protection (sustained — environmental resistance), Enhanced Trait (self — pushing physical limits over extended periods).

This tree is the physical stamina foundation. Where Durability (combat petal) handles hit absorption, Endurance handles prolonged exertion, forced marches, sleep deprivation, and environmental exposure. The transmission is sustained Protection against the world’s passive damage rather than reactive Protection against active attacks.

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.ak2ga6fgz28p"></a>**Brawler (General Combat)**
**Crux:** Improvised Weaponry — no penalties for attacking with improvised weapons. Treat improvised weapons as simple weapons for prerequisites.

**Channel Opens:** Damage (through any available object), Movement (thrown improvised weapons via Deadeye Improviser).

The Brawler tree turns ANY physical object into a viable transmission cable. Where other styles require specific weapon types, the Brawler transmits Damage through whatever is at hand — chairs, bottles, tools, environmental debris. This is the Setting B street fighter’s tree.

-----
# <a name="_heading=h.qoebjuaijcci"></a>**PART II: MENTAL PILLAR UTILITY TREES**
## <a name="_heading=h.97n5fx62yryt"></a>**Alchemy (Artifice)**
**Crux:** Alchemical Training — unlocks ability to create alchemical items and potions.

**Channel Opens:** Create (alchemical compounds), Transform (material state changes), Damage (bombs/splash weapons), Affliction (poisons), Healing (potions), Enhanced Trait (mutagens — via Mutagenic Formula cross-tree).

|Talent|Effect Decomposition|
| :- | :- |
|Alchemical Training|Create (Crux — opens the alchemical channel). No recipes granted, just the transmission capability.|
|Efficient Brewer|Create + Chronos (self — halved crafting time). The alchemist’s process accelerates through expertise.|
|Infused Concoctions|Create + Variable (bounded, CML 1) — adding a single magical “word” to an alchemical creation. Cross-medium transmission: item substrate carries a magical expression.|
|Poisoner’s Craft|Affliction (Crux — opens the toxicology sub-channel). Foundation for all poison creation.|
|Master Toxicologist|Affliction (enhanced — increased TN to resist). The poison’s transmission becomes harder to block.|
|Magnum Opus|Create (Master capstone). Auto-succeed identification. Unlocks Philosopher’s Stone project. Mastery 10 + Artifice 10 prerequisite — the peak of alchemical transmission.|

**Setting B Application:** Kan-tech chemical engineering IS alchemy transmitted through industrial process rather than mystical tradition. The Sovereign Exchange and Stormpeak both operate alchemical manufacturing at industrial scale. The Alchemy tree’s transmission specifications are identical whether the practitioner is a robed scholar or a factory chemist.

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.fsujvdd6d9w6"></a>**Mechanics / Trapmaking (Artifice)**
**Crux (Mechanics):** Intuitive Mechanic — add half Mastery to checks for understanding, repairing, or disabling mechanical devices. Full Mastery if the device originates from a familiar culture/tradition.

**Crux (Trapmaking):** Trapmaking — unlocks ability to craft and set mundane traps.

**Channel Opens:** Nullify (disabling devices/traps), Create (building traps/mechanisms), Senses (reading device architecture), Healing (Jury-Rig — temporary item repair).

|Talent|Effect Decomposition|
| :- | :- |
|Intuitive Mechanic|Senses (enhanced, Artifice-routed) — reading mechanical systems. Cultural familiarity doubles the bonus — the Setting A clockmaker and the Setting B machinist both understand mechanisms, but each reads their own tradition’s devices more fluently.|
|Jury-Rig|Healing (item, temporary) — functional repair lasting Mastery rounds before the fix fails. Cheap, fast, unreliable.|
|Trapmaking|Create + Affliction linked — building devices that impose conditions on those who trigger them. The trap IS a stored expression waiting for activation.|
|Tech Savvy|Senses (enhanced, cross-skill Artifice + Lore) — identifying unfamiliar technology. Half Mastery to both skills when analyzing unknown devices.|

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.ym7y8xufvizm"></a>**Field Medic (Survival)**
**Crux:** Healer’s Hands — pool of healing power equal to derived formula. Touch to restore HP. Passive: all HP restoration doubled.

**Channel Opens:** Healing (primary — HP restoration through touch), Panacea (condition treatment), Protection (psychological anchoring — pre-treating against hazards).

|Talent|Effect Decomposition|
| :- | :- |
|Healer’s Hands|Healing (Crux — supernatural touch healing) + Enhanced Trait (passive — all healing output doubled). The body becomes a Healing transmission cable.|
|Cauterizing Draught|Healing (alternative material — alcohol as medical supply) + Affliction (minor — Sickened 1 round). Trade-off composition: heal more but impose a temporary condition.|

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.ctb4lq9f1uji"></a>**Beast Tamer (Survival)**
**Crux:** Beast Empathy — add Mastery Rating to checks involving natural animals and beasts.

**Channel Opens:** Mind Link (animal — emotional resonance with beasts), Senses (reading animal behavior), Enhanced Trait (animal companion — improving beast capabilities).

|Talent|Effect Decomposition|
| :- | :- |
|Beast Empathy|Mind Link (limited — emotional communication with animals). Not telepathy, but trained empathic resonance.|
|Gentle Hand|Mind Link (expanded — influence attitudes of animals and magical beasts with Intelligence 2 or less). Extends the empathic channel to more exotic creatures.|

This tree feeds into the Companion system (Document 4d). Beast Tamer provides the foundational channel for companion interaction; the Companion trees (Path of the Wild, etc.) provide the specific evolution and enhancement mechanics.

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.85r718oldii0"></a>**Forager (Survival)**
**Crux:** Harvest Parts (Anatomical Salvage) — increase yield of harvested creature components by Mastery Rating. Can harvest from creatures that normally provide nothing (constructs).

**Channel Opens:** Senses (material reading — identifying what a creature’s remains contain), Create (crude — processing natural materials into components), Transform (harvesting — converting creature remains into usable materials).

This tree connects directly to the Five Artifice Paths. Forager provides the RAW MATERIALS that the Artisan shapes, the Alchemist processes, and the Enchanter populates. Without Forager, crafters must purchase materials from vendors rather than harvesting from defeated creatures.

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.tqdxrnjcmsj7"></a>**Wilderness Attunement (Survival)**
Not explicitly a single Crux tree but a cluster of Survival-routed talents covering environmental navigation, weather reading, and natural hazard management. These talents unlock the Survival skill’s broadest transmission applications — the effects that make wilderness exploration mechanically meaningful rather than hand-waved.

-----
# <a name="_heading=h.vmtinc1m7s5l"></a>**PART III: SOCIAL PILLAR UTILITY TREES**
## <a name="_heading=h.p31msokdrykr"></a>**Music (Performance)**
**Crux:** Captivating Melody — Performance check vs Mental Composite Defense to fascinate creatures within 30 feet. Maximum targets = Presence score. Mind-affecting, language-independent.

**Channel Opens:** Mind Link (emotional — fascination, emotional resonance with audience), Enhanced Trait (allies — inspiration through music), Affliction (crowd conditions through sustained performance), Chronos (pacing — controlling the emotional tempo of a scene).

|Talent|Effect Decomposition|
| :- | :- |
|Captivating Melody|Mind Link (area, Burst 30ft) + Affliction (Fascinated). Language-independent — the transmission bypasses verbal processing.|
|Harmonious Performance|Enhanced Trait (allies, Teamwork) — coordinated performance amplifying the output. When two musicians play together, the combined transmission exceeds either alone.|
|Lingering Performance|Chronos (duration extension) — performance effects persist for half-Mastery rounds after cessation. The signal echoes after transmission stops.|

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.tlus41hmso0j"></a>**Oratory (Performance)**
**Crux:** Master Orator — add Mastery to Performance checks influencing groups of 10+ people.

**Channel Opens:** Mind Link (crowd — mass persuasion), Senses (reading audience), Enhanced Trait (allies — inspiring speech), Illusion (constructing compelling narratives).

| Talent                 | Effect Decomposition                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| :--------------------- | :---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Master Orator          | Enhanced Trait (self, conditional — bonus when addressing crowds of 10+). The orator’s transmission amplifies with audience size.                                                                                 |
| Compelling Voice       | Mind Link (area, 30ft) + Affliction (Compelled to listen, Will save). Verbal transmission forces attention — targets must listen for at least one round.                                                          |
| Intelligible Oration   | Senses (output — universal comprehension). Speech understood regardless of language. Supernatural. The transmission bypasses linguistic barriers entirely.                                                        |
| Masterwork Performance | Create (permanent) — art that generates persistent inspirational effects. A masterpiece IS a permanent item transmitting Enhanced Trait continuously. Master-tier capstone requiring Performance 10 + Mastery 10. |

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.cbm08nbij6vr"></a>**Deception / Acting (Influence + Performance)**
**Crux:** Master of Deceit — add Mastery to Influence (lying/bluffing) and Performance (disguise).

**Channel Opens:** Illusion (primary — false persona, false intent, false narrative), Senses (reading whether deception is landing), Affliction (Charmed — lowering target’s guard through manufactured trust).

|Talent|Effect Decomposition|
| :- | :- |
|Master of Deceit|Illusion (enhanced — bonus to creating and maintaining false impressions). The deception channel transmits more convincingly.|
|Disarming Demeanor|Illusion (passive — appearing innocent/harmless/unassuming). Sustained low-level Illusion expression that doesn’t require an action.|
|Magical Artifice|Illusion (item-targeted) — tricking a magic item into functioning for you by emulating required qualities. Skullduggery/Influence routed through the item’s authentication system.|

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.dtttfm5s1hff"></a>**Diplomacy (Influence)**
**Crux:** Force of Personality — add Mastery to all Influence skill checks.

**Channel Opens:** Enhanced Trait (self — amplified social transmission), Mind Link (negotiation — establishing mutual understanding), Damage (Social — through diplomatic pressure), Protection (social — defending position through reasoned argument).

|Talent|Effect Decomposition|
| :- | :- |
|Force of Personality|Enhanced Trait (self, permanent — Influence channel baseline amplified). The diplomat’s every social expression hits harder.|
|Gilded Influence|Enhanced Trait (self, conditional — bonus when leveraging apparent wealth). Lore or Streetwise feeding into Influence. Cross-skill amplification.|
|Gilded Tongue|Weaken (target’s resistance to bribery) — reducing the “price” needed to achieve cooperation. Every 5 points above defense reduces bribe cost by 10%.|
|Master Negotiator|Enhanced Trait (self, conditional — bonus during negotiation/mediation). Influence + Insight dual-channel amplification.|

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.hc7thqf2rnkq"></a>**Thievery (Skullduggery)**
**Crux:** Nimble Fingers — add Mastery to disable device, pick lock, and pickpocket checks.

**Channel Opens:** Nullify (lock/device disabling), Move Object (pickpocketing — relocating small objects without detection), Senses (identifying security measures), Create (forgery — Master Forger).

|Talent|Effect Decomposition|
| :- | :- |
|Nimble Fingers|Enhanced Trait (self, permanent — Skullduggery channel for precision manipulation amplified).|
|Deft Stow|Movement (item — free action to sheathe/stow). The body moves items without consuming tempo.|
|Master Forger|Create (document — convincing forgeries). Cross-skill with Lore for subject-relevant bonus. The forger transmits Illusion through physical medium (paper).|
|Juggling|Movement (items, multiple — juggling Finesse-count objects) + Illusion (concealing weapon draw within performance). Cross-tree with Performance.|

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.wg5tob6jl0hj"></a>**Infiltration (Skullduggery)**
**Crux:** Go Unnoticed (Lost in the Crowd) — hide with only one penalty regardless of observers. Can hide without cover/concealment in crowds or distracting environments.

**Channel Opens:** Illusion (primary — stealth, invisibility through environmental blending), Protection (self — avoiding detection as defense), Senses (counter-surveillance — detecting who is watching you).

|Talent|Effect Decomposition|
| :- | :- |
|Go Unnoticed|Illusion (enhanced — reduced penalties, extended conditions for hiding). The stealth channel ignores observer quantity.|
|Featherfoot|Protection (passive — Mastery added to Dodge vs traps) + Movement (special — full speed across pressure plates without triggering). Illusion applied to weight/presence.|
|Hide in Plain Sight|Illusion (mastery — hide while directly observed, in dim light/shadow). Supernatural. The stealth channel transmits even under observation. Mastery 7+ prerequisite.|
|Enigmatic Presence|Protection (passive — Mastery added to TN to resist investigation about you). Counter-intelligence through natural mysteriousness.|

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.jd3cy3smn4t5"></a>**Urban Navigation (Streetwise)**
**Crux:** (implicit through Urban Scavenger)

**Channel Opens:** Senses (urban — finding resources, reading city infrastructure), Create (scavenging — producing items from urban debris), Movement (knowing shortcuts, hidden paths).

|Talent|Effect Decomposition|
| :- | :- |
|Urban Scavenger|Create (conditional — “find” mundane items in cities). Pool of Scavenge Points = Streetwise + tree talents. Regenerates 1 per 10 minutes searching. The urban environment IS the material source.|

-----
# <a name="_heading=h.j171t888oxkz"></a>**PART IV: METAPHYSICAL PILLAR UTILITY TREES**
## <a name="_heading=h.j4zwz0n2qmbh"></a>**Spellcraft (Metaphysics)**
**Crux:** Knowledgeable Spellcaster (Rote Caster) — choose one magical “word” (Effect or Subtype). When composing a spell including that word, effective Mastery is +1. Can be taken multiple times.

**Channel Opens:** Enhanced Trait (self, conditional — amplified Metaphysical transmission for specific Effects/Subtypes). Each purchase deepens one channel’s fidelity.

This tree represents magical SPECIALIZATION — the opposite of Variable’s breadth. Where the Praxis Collegium values flexibility, Spellcraft values depth. A caster with Focus (Fire) + Rote Caster (Damage) + Rote Caster (Fire) transmits Fire Damage at effectively Mastery+3 compared to an unspecialized caster.

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.hc3kt7i70qhk"></a>**Chameleon (General)**
**Crux:** Aptitude Focus — gain a single “floating” talent slot. Once per 10 minutes, meditate for 1 minute to fill with any talent you meet prerequisites for. Supernatural.

**Channel Opens:** Variable (bounded — replicating any talent’s transmission temporarily). The Chameleon IS Martial Flexibility applied beyond the combat petal.

|Talent|Effect Decomposition|
| :- | :- |
|Aptitude Focus|Variable (talent-level — borrow any talent’s transmission parameters temporarily). Cost: 1.5× normal XP of highest-mimicable talent.|
|Adaptive Mind|Enhanced Trait (self, permanent — reduced XP costs for skills and skill-based talents by 2). The Chameleon’s learning speed is permanently accelerated.|

-----
# <a name="_heading=h.bn7x51if1889"></a>**PART V: DEFENSIVE TREES (CROSS-PILLAR)**
These trees provide pillar-specific passive defense, available to any character. Each opens a sustained Protection channel on its specific pillar.

|Increased Fortitude|Enhanced Trait (self, permanent — HP pool increased by Mastery × 2). Can be taken multiple times.|

**Crux:** Fortified Mind — add half Mastery to Mental Dodge and Deflection against mind-affecting effects.

|Talent|Effect|
| :- | :- |
|Fortified Mind|Protection (Mental, passive — enhanced defense vs psychic/mental effects)|
|Vigilant Mind|Protection (Mental, passive — immune to magical sleep, no fatigue from 24-hour wakefulness). Supernatural.|
## <a name="_heading=h.9p359ntwke0w"></a>**Social Resilience**
**Crux:** Unshakeable Poise — add half Mastery to Social Dodge and Deflection against effects targeting composure.

|Talent|Effect|
| :- | :- |
|Unshakeable Poise|Protection (Social, passive — enhanced defense vs humiliation, goading, composure attacks)|
## <a name="_heading=h.gta4c8ypttsj"></a>**Metaphysical Resilience**
**Crux:** Indomitable Essence — add half Mastery to Metaphysical Dodge and Deflection against compulsion, possession, and will-targeting effects.

|Talent|Effect|
| :- | :- |
|Indomitable Essence|Protection (Metaphysical, passive — enhanced defense vs soul corruption)|
|Pattern Purity / Innocent Blood|Protection (Metaphysical, enhanced — full Mastery bonus vs effects altering fundamental nature)|
## <a name="_heading=h.bup8b9yqfms9"></a>**General Resilience**
**Crux:** Unyielding Spirit — do not fall unconscious at 0 HP. Continue acting while HP is above negative Vigor. Staggered while in negative range.

|Talent|Effect|
| :- | :- |
|Unyielding Spirit|Immunity (partial — death’s door resistance). Extends the functional range below 0 HP.|
|Indomitable Will|Enhanced Trait (self, conditional — no longer Staggered while in negative HP range). Full action capacity even at death’s door.|
## <a name="_heading=h.91s426li4590"></a>**Tactical Awareness**
**Crux:** Instinctive Evasion — never lose Dodge Defense when flat-footed or attacked by hidden opponent.

|Talent|Effect|
| :- | :- |
|Instinctive Evasion|Protection (passive — Dodge defense always active). Mirrors Uncanny Dodge (Agile Defense combat tree) but available as a general talent.|
|Preternatural Reflexes|Enhanced Trait (self — improved initiative and reactive capability).|
|Always Ready|Protection (advanced — act in surprise rounds even on failed Perception, never flat-footed). Requires Instinctive Evasion + Mastery 5.|

# <a name="_heading=h.3jkfzi8il4ix"></a>**PART VI: GENERAL UTILITY (CROSS-PILLAR)**
These talents don’t belong to any specific tree but provide universal utility available to any character.

|Talent|Decomposition|
| :- | :- |
|**Always Prepared**|Create (conditional — “happen to have” mundane items). Pool of Resourcefulness Points. The Flashback mechanic’s cheaper cousin.|
|**Efficient Rest**|Chronos (self — 4 hours of sleep = 8 hours of rest). Supernatural. Compresses recovery time.|
|**Expanded Reserves**|Enhanced Trait (self — Stamina pool increased by Mastery). Can be taken multiple times. Raw FP pool expansion.|
|**Quick Draw**|Movement (item — draw weapon as free action). Removes the tempo cost of weapon deployment.|
|**Swift Performance**|Chronos (self — start Performance abilities as move action instead of standard). Compresses activation time.|
|**Swift Production**|Chronos (self — craft at double speed). Requires Artifice 5, Mastery 5. Compresses crafting time.|
|**Tactical Assessment**|Senses (combat — Lore/Insight check as move action to gain attack bonus). Knowledge informing combat transmission. Cross-petal bridge.|
|**Well-Travelled**|Enhanced Trait (self — untrained Lore checks at half Mastery, trained Lore at effective +2 Mastery). The generalist’s knowledge breadth.|
|**Worldly Scholar**|Senses (languages — learn additional languages equal to Mastery) + Enhanced Trait (self — half Mastery to cultural etiquette checks).|
|**Unfettered Casting**|Nullify (self — negate detriment requiring physical spell components). Frees the magical transmission from physical constraints.|
|**Unfettered Speech**|Senses (output — speak normally regardless of physical form). Prerequisite: shapeshift capability. Communication transmission survives Transform.|

The remaining talent documents will cover the Philosophy petal (Book of Shadows magical trees — Document 4c), the Five Artifice Paths (crafting trees — Document 4d), and the Companion system trees (Document 4e).*
# <a name="_heading=h.lcf3rik03i1i"></a>**THE ÆTHERIUM CODEX — THE PHILOSOPHY PETAL**
## <a name="_heading=h.wrpmsd8zsnb9"></a>**Document 4c: Magical Talent Trees**
### <a name="_heading=h.j8644pqktll5"></a>**Canonical Draft — April 16, 2026**
-----
## <a name="_heading=h.7pibncf2ym5q"></a>**PREAMBLE**
The Philosophy petal governs magical expression through the ætheric transmission medium. Where the combat petal opens physical transmission channels through the body, and the utility trees open investigation/social/survival channels, the Philosophy petal opens channels for deliberate Spell Sentence composition — the formal grammar of magic.

Source material: Book of Shadows V6, SRD v3 Amendments 8-10, talent manual (spellcasting sections).

-----
# <a name="_heading=h.bqen0jmehdkn"></a>**PART I: CASTING PHILOSOPHIES (THE TWO PATHS)**
Before individual talent trees, every caster has a fundamental orientation that determines how they interact with the Spell Sentence grammar. This is not a talent purchase — it’s a character identity choice at creation.
## <a name="_heading=h.osnvg9isx2ik"></a>**The Architect (Studied Magic)**
**Primary Attribute:** Intellect

**How they transmit:** The Architect designs optimized spell recipes — fixed compositions that produce maximum efficiency. Their spell IS the blueprint. They cast exactly what they designed, no improvisation.

**Mechanical Benefits:** - Recipe Optimization: ESL 1-4 recipes get -4 Final Rank reduction. ESL 5-7 get -3. ESL 8-10 get -2. This means lower ÆP costs for pre-designed spells. - Complexity Mastery: Bonus to Construction Pool equal to Dominion / 2. More geometry available per spell. - Metamagic Efficiency: Intellect-based Metamagic cost reduction. Each Metamagic surcharge reduced by up to M\_int, minimum +1 ÆP per talent.

**Weakness:** Tactical rigidity. Cannot alter spell components on the fly. Changing a spell requires returning to the research phase and designing a new recipe.

**Setting Expression:** This is the Kan-tech engineer’s approach to magic. Systematize it, optimize it, manufacture it. Kanon’s philosophy embodied — every expression has measurable parameters.
## <a name="_heading=h.eyuv6dhcxiol"></a>**The Improviser (Innate Magic)**
**Primary Attribute:** Anima

**How they transmit:** The Improviser treats spell recipes as familiar melodies to riff on. They freely swap Additives and Primary Subtypes mid-cast, adapting the expression to the moment.

**Mechanical Benefits:** - On-the-Fly Modification: Can swap Additives or Primary Subtype within a recipe during casting. New combination must be valid and within Construction Pool. - Metamagic Affinity: All Metamagic Talent ÆP surcharges reduced by 2 (minimum 1). - Per-Spell ÆP Reduction: Anima-based cost reduction on individual castings.

**Weakness:** Inefficient design. No Final Rank reduction. Their base spells cost more ÆP than an Architect’s equivalent.

**Setting Expression:** This is the Setting A tradition — intuitive, personal, artistic. The sorcerer who FEELS the æther rather than measuring it.
## <a name="_heading=h.pe9coktyyqvm"></a>**The Polymath**
A character can learn both paths, maintaining separate repertoires — optimized Architect recipes AND flexible Improviser forms. The Polymath is the rarest and most versatile practitioner. They understand the full dualistic nature of the Prima Æther.

**Setting Expression:** The Praxis Collegium produces the most Polymaths — their philosophy explicitly values understanding all approaches to magic rather than committing to one.

-----
# <a name="_heading=h.omxywftp8pm3"></a>**PART II: METAMAGIC TALENTS**
These are active talents that modify spell expressions at the moment of casting by spending additional ÆP. They represent the caster’s ability to push their compositions beyond the recipe’s original design.

In Spell Sentence terms, Metamagic talents are EXTERNAL-TEMPORARY modifiers (SRD v3 Three-Layer Modifier Architecture). They don’t change the rote internally — they augment it per-casting.
## <a name="_heading=h.zheqg6hquh7l"></a>**The Metamagic Catalogue**

|Talent|ÆP Surcharge|Effect|Spell Sentence Decomposition|
| :- | :- | :- | :- |
|**Empower Spell**|+2|All variable numeric effects increased by 50%|Enhanced Trait applied to the spell’s own output — the expression’s amplitude is boosted|
|**Maximize Spell**|+4|All variable numeric effects treated as maximum value|Enhanced Trait (maximum) — the expression’s variance is eliminated, output is ceiling|
|**Quicken Spell**|+(ESL × 2)|Standard action casting becomes swift/minor action|Chronos (self) — the composition time is compressed. Tempo cost drops from Standard to Light.|
|**Enlarge Spell**|+1|Double the range|Construction Pool: Range parameter doubled. A Close spell becomes Medium-equivalent reach.|
|**Widen Spell**|+2|Double the area of effect|Construction Pool: Target/Shape parameter doubled. A Burst (small) becomes Burst (medium).|
|**Extend Spell**|+1|Double the duration|Construction Pool: Duration parameter doubled. Minutes becomes Hours-equivalent.|
|**Unbalanced Magic**|—|Two combat maneuvers available when casting attack spells|Two modes: Overwhelm (penalty to attack, bonus to save DC/damage) or Pinpoint (penalty to damage/DC, bonus to attack). Trade-off within the same expression — shifting energy between accuracy and amplitude.|

**Architect Efficiency:** Each Metamagic surcharge is reduced by up to M\_int (Intellect-derived modifier), minimum +1 ÆP per talent. A high-Intellect Architect applies Metamagic cheaply because their theoretical understanding optimizes the modification.

**Improviser Affinity:** All Metamagic surcharges reduced by flat 2, minimum 1. Improvisers are naturally gifted at pushing limits — their intuitive connection to æther makes modification instinctive.

-----
# <a name="_heading=h.1501987be5sd"></a>**PART III: FOCUS TALENTS**
These are passive talents representing deep affinity for specific magical domains. They permanently enhance the caster’s transmission fidelity for chosen Effects or Subtypes.

In Spell Sentence terms, Focus talents are EXTERNAL-PERMANENT modifiers. They don’t change the rote — they improve everything routed through that channel.
## <a name="_heading=h.jwlvz4850yft"></a>**The Focus Catalogue**

|Talent|Effect|Can Take Multiple Times?|
| :- | :- | :- |
|**Focus (Effect)**|Choose one Effect (e.g., Affliction). All spells using this Effect have their save DC increased by 1.|Yes — each purchase for a different Effect, or stacking on the same Effect|
|**Focus (Subtype)**|Choose one Primary Subtype (e.g., Fire / B-01 Inferno). All spells with this Subtype have their save DC increased by 1.|Yes — same stacking rules|
|**Spell Penetration**|Bonus on checks to overcome magical resistance or counter-magic (Nullify).|No — but prerequisite for Greater Spell Penetration|
|**Warden Breaker**|Significant bonus to overcome static magical defenses (wards, Deepguard barriers). Specialist counter-abjuration.|No|
|**Immunity Breach**|Prerequisite: High Overall Mastery. By spending significant additional ÆP, temporarily suppress a creature’s Immunity to a specific Subtype for one round.|No — Mastery talent, requires deep investment|

**The Specialization Curve:** A caster stacking Focus (Effect: Damage) + Focus (Subtype: B-01 Inferno) + Rote Caster (Damage) + Rote Caster (Fire) transmits Inferno Damage at effectively +4 over an unspecialized caster. This IS the optimization depth the 3.5e community expects — meaningful mechanical reward for focused investment.

-----
# <a name="_heading=h.3kptzt5pxp97"></a>**PART IV: POOL MANAGEMENT TALENTS**
## <a name="_heading=h.rrbl3hs3whxv"></a>**Expanded Consciousness**
**Effect:** Each purchase adds (Overall Mastery × 3) to your Mastery Capacity Pool (ÆP maximum).

**Purchase Limit:** Maximum purchases = Overall Mastery ÷ 2 (rounded down).

**XP Cost:** Starts at 5, increases linearly with each purchase.

**Spell Sentence Decomposition:** This isn’t an effect — it’s a raw transmission capacity expansion. More ÆP = more fuel = more expressions per day. The specialist Summoner or controller who needs massive pools to sustain multiple concurrent expressions buys deep into Expanded Consciousness.

**Scaling Example (from Book of Shadows):** - Mastery 9 caster with 6 purchases: Base 24 + (6 × 18) = 132 ÆP - Same caster at Mastery 10 with 7 purchases: Base 28 + (7 × 21) = 175 ÆP - The pool grows multiplicatively with both Mastery advancement and talent purchases

-----
# <a name="_heading=h.agpn4nx9xqzb"></a>**PART V: HYBRID COMBAT-MAGIC TREES**
These trees bridge the combat petal and philosophy petal — they unlock channels for composing Spell Sentence expressions THROUGH physical combat actions simultaneously.
## <a name="_heading=h.yt76uiul4bbl"></a>**Path of the Spellblade (Magus)**
The quintessential martial-magical fusion. Channel rotes through weapon strikes.

|Tier|Talent|Effect|
| :- | :- | :- |
|**Entry**|Combat Casting|No concentration checks while threatened. The caster can compose Spell Sentence expressions in melee without disruption. Removes the “laboratory not battlefield” restriction on Metaphysics.|
|**Tier 1**|Spellstrike|Channel a touch-based rote through a melee weapon. If the attack hits, it delivers weapon damage AND the rote’s effect simultaneously. Linked Damage + [any touch-range Effect]. Small ÆP activation cost.|
|**Tier 2**|Spell Combat|During a full attack action, cast one rote AND make weapon attacks. Penalty to all attack rolls for the round. Linked [full attack Damage] + [any rote with standard-or-less casting time]. Stamina cost.|
|**Capstone**|Improved Spell Combat|No penalty on attack rolls when using Spell Combat. Perfect synthesis — ætheric and physical transmission operate simultaneously without interference.|

**Transmission Framework:** The Spellblade treats the weapon as a DUAL-MEDIUM cable — carrying both physical expression (Damage through material) and ætheric expression (rote through formal composition) simultaneously. Most characters transmit through only one medium per action. The Spellblade transmits through both.
## <a name="_heading=h.egrf0voda5jn"></a>**Path of the Shaped Veil (Akashic)**
Shape raw æther into semi-permanent constructs worn on the body. Investment-based sustained expressions.

|Component|Mechanic|
| :- | :- |
|**Shape Veil (Entry)**|Shape soul-energy into a Veil occupying a body slot (head, hands, feet, etc.). Limited number active simultaneously.|
|**Æther Investment**|Each Veil is powered by investing Stamina/Æther from your pool. More investment = stronger expression. A Veil at 1 point invested provides minor benefit; at maximum investment it provides major benefit.|
|**Reallocation**|Shift investment between active Veils as a Light action (1 tempo). Mid-combat tactical decision — more defense or more offense?|
|**Veil Katas**|Each new Veil learned is a separate talent purchase. Specific Veils provide specific effects (Gorgon’s Gaze = Affliction + Intimidation, Dragon Mantle = Damage + Protection).|

**Transmission Framework:** The Veil IS a sustained expression bound to a body slot. It’s not a spell being cast — it’s a permanent-until-dismissed transmission channel that requires continuous FP fuel. The Akashic practitioner doesn’t cast and forget; they maintain a dynamic portfolio of active expressions, redistributing fuel in real time.
## <a name="_heading=h.u06rm57dixfl"></a>**Path of the Unbroken Blade (Sublime Way / Path of War)**
Structured martial arts as learnable, expendable Katas with recovery mechanics.

|Component|Mechanic|
| :- | :- |
|**Learn Discipline (Entry)**|Gain access to a specific martial Discipline (e.g., Steel Serpent, Primal Fury). Learn a number of Katas and Stances from that discipline.|
|**Kata Expenditure**|Spend Stamina to perform Katas. Once used, a Kata is expended until recovered. This IS the Spell Sentence composed through physical medium with a “prepared slots” limitation.|
|**Recovery Mechanic**|Each Discipline has a unique recovery method (e.g., full action to regain all expended Katas, or recover one Kata per successful attack). The recovery IS the drip recharge for physical-medium expressions.|
|**Stances**|Sustained postures granting passive bonuses. Only one active at a time. [Stance] tag. These are sustained Protection/Enhanced Trait expressions.|

**Transmission Framework:** The Sublime Way treats physical technique as PREPARED EXPRESSIONS — like an Architect’s recipes but for the body. You prepare a loadout of Katas, spend them during combat, then recover them through your Discipline’s specific recharge method. This bridges the gap between “combat talent as channel opener” and “spell recipe as prepared expression.”
## <a name="_heading=h.i1h2bnax6kxq"></a>**Arcane Strike / Sōmaturgic Edge**
**Effect:** As a swift/minor action, channel energy into your weapon. For 1 round, attacks deal extra damage equal to Mastery tier bonus. Innate ability — does not cost ÆP.

**Transmission Framework:** The simplest hybrid expression. Your weapon temporarily carries a Damage + Enhanced Trait (weapon) composition through ætheric medium, powered by your innate connection rather than formal casting. The Ætherforged Precepts’ foundational cross-medium talent.
## <a name="_heading=h.6h3tfngfxte6"></a>**Armored Casting**
**Effect:** Tiered talent. Rank 1: ignore Ritual Components (-1) Subtractive drawbacks in light armor. Rank 2: ignore (-1) and (-2) in medium armor.

**Transmission Framework:** Armor normally dampens ætheric transmission (the physical medium interferes with the ætheric channel). This talent trains the caster to transmit through both media simultaneously despite the interference — like learning to sing clearly while wearing a heavy coat.

-----
# <a name="_heading=h.vl603awqwkrk"></a>**PART VI: THE ÆP POOL FORMULA**
The unified ÆP pool that fuels all Philosophy petal expressions:

**ÆP Pool = (Dominion + Tₘ) × Mastery**

Where Tₘ = total number of magical/supernatural talents purchased.

This formula is philosophy-agnostic — Architects and Improvisers use the same pool. Increasing Dominion or buying more magical talents both increase ÆP linearly. No hard cap on Tₘ.

**The Critical Interaction:** Every talent purchased across the Philosophy petal increases Tₘ, which increases the total ÆP pool. This means a character who buys deep into Focus talents and Metamagic talents has a LARGER ÆP pool than one who doesn’t — the investment in specialization also funds more fuel. The system rewards magical investment with increased capacity.

-----
# <a name="_heading=h.cod7xo96z1ng"></a>**PART VII: COLLEGE SIGNATURE EFFECTS**
Each of the nine colleges has a signature magical approach (SRD v3 Amendment 10):

|College|Signature Effect|Planar Home|
| :- | :- | :- |
|Chiaroscuro Institute|Illusion|Palimpsest|
|Chronosynclastic Institute|Chronos + Senses|Palimpsest / Synod interface|
|Deepguard Academy|Protection + Nullify|Hidden Forge|
|Mortis Ævum Collegium|Affliction + Weaken + Healing (entropic)|Synod|
|Praxis Collegium|Variable|None (isotope-independent)|
|Sovereign Exchange|Create + Enhanced Trait|Borderlands / Hidden Forge|
|Stormpeak Academy|Damage|Borderlands|
|Unity Academy|Mind Link|Palimpsest boundary|
|Wildpath Wardens|Transform|Verdant Font|

A student of a specific college invests their Focus talents in their college’s signature effects and subtypes. The college’s curriculum IS the recommended Focus/Metamagic/Expanded Consciousness investment path. The system doesn’t force this — a Stormpeak student CAN take Focus (Illusion) — but the institutional support, training resources, and mentorship align with the signature.

This mirrors the 18 Ætherforged Precepts as assembly blueprints (Document 4). The Precepts recommend combat tree combinations. The colleges recommend Philosophy tree combinations. Both are guidelines, not restrictions.

-----
*End of Document 4c. This document covers the Philosophy petal — magical talent trees from the Book of Shadows. The remaining talent documents will cover the Five Artifice Paths (crafting trees — Document 4d) and the Companion system trees (Document 4e).*
# <a name="_heading=h.pe1zlumk59xx"></a>**THE ÆTHERIUM CODEX — CRAFTING & COMPANION TREES**
## <a name="_heading=h.n6vifaytwiwf"></a>**Document 4d: Specialized Petal Talents**
### <a name="_heading=h.6377ixjzonsh"></a>**Canonical Draft — April 16, 2026**
-----
## <a name="_heading=h.jbqvsirnc8qb"></a>**PREAMBLE**
