# <a name="_heading=h.vq1g5611is4w"></a>**PART V: FORMATION STAGE AS ENVIRONMENTAL ESL**
## <a name="_heading=h.pxqi08g9gxok"></a>**The Parallel**
Formation Stage IS ESL for environmental expressions. The local Formation Stage determines the environment’s expression power.

|Formation Stage|Equivalent|Material Tier|Description|
| :- | :- | :- | :- |
|1-4|Novice|Tier 1: Resonant|Background effects. Faint impressions. Witchwood, Humming Stone.|
|5-7|Journeyman|Tier 2: True|Active effects. Combat-relevant hazards. Glimmersteel, Singing Stone, Ignis Coal.|
|8-10|Master|Tier 3: Prime|Dominant effects. The environment IS the encounter. Orichalcum, Living Obsidian, Void-Iron.|
## <a name="_heading=h.mdhcewqtec1"></a>**The Radiating Zone Model**
A planar bleed source radiates outward in concentric rings of decreasing intensity. Boundaries between rings are STAGGERED — local geology creates pockets of higher or lower intensity within each ring based on resonance compatibility.

|Zone|Formation Stage|What Forms|Biological Effect|
| :- | :- | :- | :- |
|**Core**|8-10|Prime materials|Extreme mutation pressure. Creatures are powerful but unstable — high mutation count, deep XP debt, ecological niches in active formation.|
|**Inner Ring**|5-7|True materials|Significant mutation pressure. Established species showing new traits. New species appearing. The frontier.|
|**Outer Ring**|3-4|Resonant materials|Mild mutation pressure. Existing species with minor adaptations. Materials slightly enhanced above mundane.|
|**Periphery**|1-2|Mundane with faint signature|Negligible mutation pressure. Ecology normal with occasional anomalies. Detectable by Survival/Insight.|
## <a name="_heading=h.qhaqtx7h7q9m"></a>**Mechanical Procedure for Entering a Bleed Zone**
When a party enters an environmental zone, the effects are governed by the two-axis model:

**Axis 1 — EML (Intensity):** How powerful is the planar pressure? Determines what happens per tick — how much strain accumulates. Higher EML = faster, more dramatic adaptation/mutation.

**Axis 2 — Scope (Duration/Area):** How long has this been active and how wide? Determines how OFTEN the pressure ticks relative to the characters’ frame.

**The tick rate:** An EML X effect at Scope Y ticks at every S(Y-X) interval: - EML 8 at Scope 8: ticks every S0 tempo (~seconds). Immediate, overwhelming pressure. - EML 4 at Scope 8: ticks every S4 interval (~weeks). Slow, sustained pressure. - EML 2 at Scope 4: ticks every S2 interval (~hours). Moderate pace.

**What happens on each tick:** - The environment makes its “attack” — the isotope profile’s natural effects at the current Formation Stage apply to everything in the zone. - Characters resist through Praxis (soul’s immune system) for mutation pressure, Survival for environmental hazards, and appropriate pillar defense for direct effects. - Strain accumulates per the SRD v3 Adaptation Pressure formula: Formation Stage × 2. - The specific effects are determined by the isotope profile — read the Formation Stage Shift for the dominant isotope. What the SRD v3 describes as item properties AT that stage is ALSO what the terrain does at that stage.

-----
# <a name="_heading=h.w42iyiz91cik"></a>**PART VI: THE PALIMPSEST — A SPECIAL CASE**
## <a name="_heading=h.pjysi8mxsc5"></a>**Not Thinning — Bunching**
The Palimpsest does NOT bleed like the other planes. Where Borderlands bleed THINS the boundary and material leaks through, the Palimpsest BUNCHES UP. A Palimpsest “bleed” is the probability substrate becoming locally DENSER, not thinner.

**What this means:** Palimpsest bleeds don’t produce material environmental effects (no isotope-specific terrain hazards like Inferno damage or Mire adhesion). They produce NARRATIVE and PROBABILITY effects. Events become more dramatic. Coincidences multiply. The dice feel weighted. Outcomes cluster toward narrative satisfaction rather than statistical distribution.

**The Smoothing Effect IS a Palimpsest overlay.** The Smoothing Effect works BECAUSE it’s the Palimpsest’s probability management applied to the entire setting. The Smoothing Effect enables actual decisions to collapse into reality without being overwhelmed by chaos. Without it, every possible outcome would be equally real simultaneously — the bunching would become total, and causality would dissolve.

**Controlled Palimpsest access is safe.** Bags of holding, rope trick, extradimensional spaces — these are controlled folds in the Palimpsest. They’re pinches in the fabric that create accessible pockets. They don’t cause bleed because they’re engineered to be self-contained.

**Uncontrolled Palimpsest bunching IS a bleed.** When the Palimpsest bunches without engineering, probability goes haywire locally. This explains the Maestro phenomenon — the narrative weight of the Palimpsest bunching up, making stories MORE potent locally. Wild magic surges, prophetic visions, impossible coincidences, dramatic reversals.

**Navigating the Palimpsest:** Because the Palimpsest is the inverted concept of the choices that DIDN’T collapse into Tessera, its internal landscape is always a Setting A or Setting B segregation. You can’t simply exit by walking 10 feet — the “landscape” isn’t contiguous with Tessera’s geography. You need to find an exit (a fold boundary, a portal, or navigate through the uncollapsed probability space). The Fey Wilds and Shadow equivalents from other settings are aspects of this un-collapsed landscape.

-----
# <a name="_heading=h.tyl4jaotngsv"></a>**PART VII: TEMPORAL VARIATION — MAGICAL WEATHER PATTERNS**
## <a name="_heading=h.ai95ejkuknol"></a>**Planes as Tectonic Plates**
The five planes are not static overlays. They MOVE — like tectonic plates or floating icebergs. Their positions relative to Tessera’s geography shift over time, creating:

**Seasonal patterns:** Some regions experience intensified bleed during specific seasons because the relevant plane’s “plate” is closer. A coastal region might experience intensified Borderlands Water-dominant bleed during winter (storms, flooding, marine creature activity) and reduced bleed during summer.

**Orbital mechanics:** The planes have their own dynamics — the Selûnae (Triple Moon Goddesses) represent lunar cycles that affect Palimpsest density. Full moons may correlate with increased probability bunching. Eclipse events may produce brief Emulsion-state environmental conditions.

**Geological interaction:** Just as Earth’s weather results from atmospheric currents interacting with terrain (Tornado Alley from Great Plains geography, hurricanes from warm ocean currents, tsunamis from tectonic events, the Sahara from Hadley cell dynamics, Everest’s death zone from altitude + jet stream), Tessera’s magical weather results from planar currents interacting with local geology.

**Real-world parallels in Tessera:**

|Terrestrial Pattern|Tessera Equivalent|
| :- | :- |
|Tornado Alley|A corridor where two opposing planar bleeds collide regularly — turbulent flow zone producing violent environmental expression surges|
|Hurricane Season|Periodic intensification of oceanic Borderlands bleed during specific planar orbital alignment|
|Tsunami|Sudden acute planar tear producing a wave of environmental expression that propagates outward from the breach point|
|Sahara Formation|Long-term Synod dominant bleed desiccating a region over centuries — geological-scale environmental clock|
|Everest Weather|High-altitude zone where atmospheric Borderlands bleed is intensified by geological elevation — extreme Formation Stage exposure|
## <a name="_heading=h.k3obreef94y8"></a>**The Weather Forecast**
The colleges track environmental conditions the way meteorologists track physical weather. The Chronosynclastic Institute’s temporal focus gives them the best “forecasting” capability — reading probability trends (Palimpsest) overlaid on entropic patterns (Synod) to predict where bleed intensification will occur. This IS their institutional function: environmental intelligence.

The Wildpath Wardens provide the ecological monitoring — tracking how current bleed patterns affect biological systems. The Deepguard provides containment forecasting — predicting where Reality Seams might form or destabilize. The Stormpeak Command provides the raw measurement infrastructure — their mesa position gives them atmospheric Borderlands data that feeds all other colleges’ models.

-----
# <a name="_heading=h.b0x7ydurrm66"></a>**PART VIII: REALITY SEAMS**
## <a name="_heading=h.92kpj9i15kso"></a>**What They Are**
Reality Seams are locations where the dissolved solution is locally disrupted — where the boundary between the merged realities became visible. They’re EDDIES in the solution, not breaks in reality.
## <a name="_heading=h.9cpf7ua86uw"></a>**Classification**

|Type|Formation Stage|Description|
| :- | :- | :- |
|**Dormant**|1-3|Stable scar. Residual isotope expression. Material harvesting opportunity. Background mutation pressure.|
|**Active**|4-6|Ongoing dissolution. Fluctuating expression. Combat-relevant environmental hazards. Significant mutation pressure.|
|**Volatile**|7-9|Fresh or intensifying disruption. The environment IS the encounter. Multiple condition track pressures. Extreme mutation risk. Prime material formation.|
|**Catastrophic**|10|The Smoothing Effect is locally overwhelmed. Civilizational-scale threat. College containment deployment. The Seam IS a campaign arc.|
## <a name="_heading=h.4690nj58k0z1"></a>**The Radiating Zones**
Seams radiate outward in the same concentric ring model as standard bleed sources. The difference: Seams often involve MULTIPLE competing isotope profiles simultaneously, producing cross-planar environmental expressions (Part IV) at the boundaries between rings.

-----
# <a name="_heading=h.7ygs239cmgv4"></a>**PART IX: THE CALAMITY’S GROUND ZERO**
The center of the Calamity — where Mortis Ævum now sits — is the oldest, most intense, and most stable Reality Seam in Tessera. It is where the Palimpsest collapsed entirely during the merge event.

**What this means:** The Synod of Endings sits transposed above this site. This isn’t metaphorical — the plane of endings is PHYSICALLY overlaid on this geography. The solidified history of the setting IS the landscape. Stories end here and reach their most stable state.

**The Soul Distribution Function:** Because the Synod is transposed here, this is where souls are distributed through the outer planes after death. The soul cycle engages through this site. The distribution is a court proceeding — the gods (or their proxies) argue their case for each soul based on the deceased’s Psyche Schema readings. Positional divinity makes this jurisdictionally complex — which god has claim depends on which divine aspects scored highest in the Schema.

**The Hot/Cold Deserts:** The two deserts flanking the oasis aren’t natural — they’re magically sustained by the Synod bleed. One represents the hot entropy of active decay (fire, dissolution, acceleration). The other represents the cold entropy of stasis (preservation, freezing, deceleration). Both are extreme Synod expressions at Formation Stage 8+. The oasis between them is the HABITABLE POCKET where the two entropic extremes cancel each other enough for life to persist.

**Mortis Ævum chose this location** because their institutional expertise IS entropy management. They study endings. They manage the soul cycle. They sit at ground zero because they’re the only college that can OPERATE in this environment — everyone else would be destroyed by the sustained Stage 8+ Synod pressure.

-----
# <a name="_heading=h.868abjnng6ej"></a>**PART X: SETTING EXPRESSIONS AND INFRASTRUCTURE**
## <a name="_heading=h.7du4iw84hc29"></a>**Three Approaches to Environmental Bleed**
Each setting expression has a distinct relationship with environmental bleed. This is the practical implication of the dissolved solution at ground level.
### <a name="_heading=h.2nl8le8f274r"></a>**Setting A Expression (Intuitive/Traditional)**
**Approach:** Work WITH the bleed. Sacred groves, ritual sites, stone circles positioned at resonance points. Architecture designed to channel and harmonize with local planar expression rather than control it. The Elder Fey are accepted as natural manifestations of the environment.

**Infrastructure:** Wards carved from local resonant materials. Ritual practices timed to seasonal bleed patterns. Agricultural cycles matched to planar orbital alignment. Medicine derived from local flora that has already adapted to the bleed.

**Vulnerability:** Dependent on environmental stability. If the bleed pattern shifts (seasonal change, planar tectonic event), Setting A infrastructure must adapt or fail.
### <a name="_heading=h.wb08lw6i24m"></a>**Setting B Expression (Engineered/Industrial)**
**Approach:** HARNESS the bleed. Kan-tech infrastructure designed to capture, channel, and exploit environmental planar expression. The bleed is a power source, not a spiritual presence. The Lost Fey are studied as phenomena, not respected as entities.

**Infrastructure:** Resonance chambers that capture bleed energy and convert it to power. Kan-tech factories sited in bleed zones specifically to harvest the environmental expression as industrial input. Communication networks routed through ley lines as data infrastructure. Military hardware designed to operate in (and weaponize) environmental conditions.

**Vulnerability:** Dependent on engineering tolerances. If the bleed intensity exceeds design specifications (Formation Stage spike during turbulent flow), Setting B infrastructure fails catastrophically — not gracefully. Kan-tech devices overload. Resonance chambers crack. The infrastructure that was designed to harness the environment becomes a hazard.
### <a name="_heading=h.pa6r0r4mc3n5"></a>**Setting C Expression (The Third Blending / Hybrid)**
**Approach:** LIVE IN the bleed. Neither working with it spiritually nor engineering it industrially, but treating it as normal — the way fish don’t notice water. The Newborn Fey are neighbors, not spirits or phenomena.

**Infrastructure:** Hybrid architecture mixing Setting A resonance principles with Setting B engineering. Kan-tech devices with ritual components. Buildings that are both mechanically sound AND spiritually aligned. This is the natural expression of a civilization that grew up IN the dissolved solution rather than experiencing it as foreign.

**Vulnerability:** Cultural Dissonance (from Document 3). Setting C populations are adapted to THEIR local blend — entering a region with a different blend profile produces the “fascinating barbarian” effect. Their hybrid approach doesn’t translate to environments dominated by pure Setting A or pure Setting B expression.

-----
# <a name="_heading=h.ffvom0v4av75"></a>**PART XI: THE FEY AS ENVIRONMENTAL OUTPUT**
## <a name="_heading=h.56filuxxgtvv"></a>**Not Creatures That Live in the Environment — Creatures the Environment PRODUCES**
Fey are born from the Palimpsest’s interaction with accumulated narrative weight. When the stories told by a culture accumulate enough mass, the Palimpsest’s probability substrate collapses portions of that narrative into physical form. A Fey IS a story that became real.
## <a name="_heading=h.bs6bo61b40ol"></a>**Three Generations**
### <a name="_heading=h.eppdxmssygs8"></a>**Elder Fey (Setting A Stories)**
Born from the original stories, parables, and primal beliefs of Setting A’s native inhabitants BEFORE the Calamity. Living manifestations of pure, undiluted nature myths, seasonal cycles, and ancient archetypes.

**Nature:** Powerful and primal but melancholic. They are stories whose world changed. A river spirit laments that her river flows through alien terrain. A sun-spirit finds the light a different “texture.” They are keepers of forgotten truths and may be hostile to the new world imposed on their old one.

**Environmental origin:** Produced by long-duration, high-intensity Setting A narrative pressure. They formed in the Palimpsest BEFORE the Calamity, when Setting A was the entire reality.
### <a name="_heading=h.s6j00k8elqaq"></a>**Lost Fey (Setting B Stories)**
Born from the folklore of the world destroyed in the Calamity. The Smoothing Effect stripped their world of physical reality but couldn’t erase its stories. The narrative echoes coalesced into beings.

**Nature:** Tragic, fragmented, obsessive. Living stories whose CONTEXT has been deleted. A knight searching for a kingdom that exists only as a name in a forgotten song. A trickster whose riddles reference stars no longer in the sky. Driven by profound loss.

**Environmental origin:** Produced by Setting B narrative weight that survived the Calamity as Palimpsest residue. They formed in the GAPS — the places where Setting B’s reality was erased but its stories persisted.
### <a name="_heading=h.bu81vhf26br6"></a>**Newborn Fey (Setting C / Third Blending Stories)**
Born from current stories, urban legends, and cultural beliefs of the merged world. The youngest and most numerous generation.

**Nature:** Dynamic, adaptable, connected to the present. They embody the dualistic nature of the merged world. Fey of the Unbroken Road. Fey of the Stonefang Mountains. Fey of the Serene City of Volition. Living folklore of the world as it IS.

**Environmental origin:** Produced by ongoing Palimpsest bunching wherever current narrative density is high enough. Cities produce more Newborn Fey than wilderness because cities concentrate narrative weight — more stories, more densely, more rapidly.
## <a name="_heading=h.d7p8dbjw6ip4"></a>**Fey as Environmental Indicator**
The Fey generation present in a region tells you what the dominant narrative pressure is. Elder Fey = this region retains strong Setting A identity. Lost Fey = Setting B’s ghost is strong here. Newborn Fey = the Third Blending has taken hold. A region with ALL THREE present is at a narrative boundary — the blending is incomplete, the solution hasn’t fully dissolved the old stories.

-----
# <a name="_heading=h.md3iagki4gf1"></a>**PART XII: THE MUTAGENIC CASCADE**
## <a name="_heading=h.x0dnllf48afq"></a>**Physical Mutation (Adaptation Through Pressure)**
Sustained exposure to environmental bleed applies Planar Strain. Strain accumulates per the Adaptation Pressure formula: Formation Stage × 2.

The bleed is magically ADAPTIVE pressure, not radiation. Higher doses don’t kill — they create creatures that are powerful but unstable. High mutation count, deep XP debt, occupying ecological niches that are actively shifting as the planar influence introduces a wildcard of external pressure.

**The Two-Axis Model:**

|Axis|What It Determines|
| :- | :- |
|**EML (Intensity)**|How much strain per tick — how dramatic each adaptation step is|
|**Scope (Duration/Area)**|How often ticks occur relative to the subject — how quickly adaptations accumulate|

**Power vs Speed Inversion:** More powerful bleeds produce faster mutation. An EML 8 Scope 8 bleed zone transforms creatures in days to weeks. An EML 2 Scope 8 bleed zone transforms populations over generations. But the EML 8 mutations are UNSTABLE (high debt, ecological flux), while the EML 2 mutations are STABLE (fully integrated, low debt, established niches).
## <a name="_heading=h.e8nlujd11rjb"></a>**Cognitive Dissonance (The Great Truth)**
Exposure to the knowledge that Tessera is artificial produces Mental condition track damage through the existing Clarity track. The immediate shock uses standard track mechanics — take Mental damage, recover with rest and treatment.

But Gnosis is CUMULATIVE and PERMANENT as a knowledge state. You can’t unknow the Great Truth. The Clarity track measures immediate cognitive shock. The Gnosis milestone measures accumulated understanding:

|Milestone|Description|Mechanical Interaction|
| :- | :- | :- |
|**Peripheral**|Something is wrong with reality but can’t articulate it|Minor Clarity track pressure on first exposure|
|**Partial**|Understands specific elements of the Great Truth|Moderate Clarity track pressure; manageable with Praxis/Medicine|
|**Full**|Comprehends the scope of the Great Lie|Severe Clarity track pressure; risk of sustained track damage|
|**Transcendent**|Integrated the Truth without being destroyed|Clarity track stabilizes; prerequisite for S7+ institutional operation|
## <a name="_heading=h.tltish56onc6"></a>**Migration and Heritage**
As long as environmental pressure continues, strain accumulates. When pressure stops, you keep what you have but stop gaining. Migration means:

•            You carry existing mutations and Heritage State

•            You stop accumulating strain from the origin bleed

•            If you enter a new bleed zone, you start accumulating from THAT bleed

•            In neutral zones: stable, no new pressure, no loss

•            Further development requires conventional means (training, alchemy, deliberate exposure)

Mutations are HERITABLE. A population’s Heritage State is the geological/planar history of their bloodline, readable like strata in a cliff face.

-----
# <a name="_heading=h.gag2e48wpgzw"></a>**PART XIII: ENVIRONMENTAL CLOCKS**
Two temporal scales of environmental tracking:
## <a name="_heading=h.lcfqj86s5zz7"></a>**Adventure-Scale Clocks (Session-to-Session)**
**Ley Line Flow State:** Tracks transitions between Laminar and Turbulent flow. Filled by accumulated stress (sustained high-ESL casting, Systemic Catalyst operations, Reality Seam proximity). Drained by Deepguard ward operations, natural settling, reduced magical activity. When filled past threshold: Laminar → Turbulent. When filled to double threshold: Turbulent → Torrential.

**Seam Activity:** Tracks whether a specific Reality Seam is Dormant, Active, Volatile, or Catastrophic. Filled by accumulated planar pressure, deliberate disruption, failed containment operations. Drained by Deepguard containment, Smoothing Effect reassertion, natural dissolution.
## <a name="_heading=h.4kffq7q5hj8g"></a>**Campaign-Scale Properties (Geological Time)**
**Formation Stage:** Not a clock — a terrain property. Changes on geological timescales (decades to centuries) unless an acute event (planar tear, catastrophic Seam) accelerates the shift. Tells the HISTORY of the land like geological strata.

**Ecological State:** The biological expression of accumulated environmental pressure. Changes on biological timescales (years to generations). The Wildpath Wardens monitor this as their institutional function.

Both campaign-scale properties are SET BY THE GM at campaign start and evolve based on campaign-scale institutional decisions, not individual adventuring actions. A party can’t change a region’s Formation Stage through S0 combat — but an institution executing an S5 operation over months can.

-----
# <a name="_heading=h.a6gipkfleaaa"></a>**PART XIV: THE COLD WAR AS ENVIRONMENTAL MANAGEMENT**
The Cold War Against Reality IS environmental management:

|Front|Environmental Responsibility|College|
| :- | :- | :- |
|**Physical Front**|Containing creatures, sealing Seams, managing ruins|Wildpath, Mortis Ævum, Stormpeak|
|**Informational Front**|Explaining anomalies to the public, narrative management|Chiaroscuro, Unity|
|**Conceptual Front**|Predicting threats, monitoring ley line patterns, timeline pruning|Chronosynclastic|
|**Containment**|Ward operations, Seam stabilization|Deepguard|
|**Resource Management**|Material distribution, economic regulation of Tier 2-3 materials|Sovereign Exchange|
|**Interstitial Monitoring**|Catching bleeds between positions, codifying cross-planar expressions|Praxis|

The commoner experiences the Cold War as “mundane leakage” — unexplained quarantines, bizarre weather, strange folklore, lucky material finds. The colleges maintain the Great Lie by explaining environmental phenomena as natural processes rather than planar bleed events.

-----
# <a name="_heading=h.i4z2ogahtlee"></a>**PART XV: THE BRIDGE TO ANCESTRY**
## <a name="_heading=h.wvegoo3t60mr"></a>**Environmental Pressure Creates Populations**
Ancestry is adaptive response to local solution chemistry. No designed races — the environment shaped what populations became.

**Setting A dominant regions** produce populations adapted to intuitive magical expression. High Anima, natural ætheric sensitivity, traditional Heritage States.

**Setting B dominant regions** produce populations adapted to Kan-tech integration. High Intellect/Acuity, natural technological affinity.

**Reality Seam regions** produce the Aether-touched — populations adapted to competing planar expressions. High Integrity (pattern resistance), mixed Heritage States, visible mutation markers.

**Palimpsest-proximate regions** produce populations adapted to probability. High Anima/Composure, natural luck modifiers.

**Bleed zone populations** carry the isotope signature of their ancestral environment. A population from a B-03 Slag zone shows fire adaptation. A population from a V-07 Spore zone shows fungal symbiosis. The Heritage State IS the environmental history of the bloodline.

-----
*End of Document 5 (Revised). The environment operates through the same engine as combat, magic, and social interaction. The geology determines resonance compatibility. The weather determines bleed intensity and distribution. The duration determines material formation and biological adaptation. Everything connects: the colleges chose their positions for resonance, the materials form from environmental conditions, the creatures adapt to sustained pressure, and the Fey are born from narrative density. Document 6 will formalize the Heritage State system that translates environmental history into playable ancestries.*
# <a name="_heading=h.16r3q4fkv9qp"></a>**ÆTHERIUM CODEX — MASTER TALENT CATALOGUE**
## <a name="_heading=h.8n8qsctu02xp"></a>**Compiled & Updated — April 15, 2026**
### <a name="_heading=h.ddarul9vhhoz"></a>**All Talents Organized by Tree, Tagged by Type, Sources Noted**
-----
**Tag Legend:** - **[Mundane]** — No magical component. Physical/mental training only. - **[Magical]** — Learned magical technique. Feeds Tₘ in ÆP pool formula. - **[Infused]** — Mundane action enhanced by magical energy (ÆP cost). - **[Supernatural]** — Innate magical ability tied to the body. Future mutation catalogue candidate. - **[Active]** — Requires an action to use. - **[Reactive]** — Uses reaction. - **[Swift]** — Uses swift/minor action. - **[Passive]** — Always on, no action required. - **[Stance]** — Persistent state, one active at a time, switch as swift action.

**Source Legend:** - **(F)** — Feat-derived (PF1e/3.5e feat conversion, April 10 pass) - **(C)** — Class-derived (class ability extraction, April 15 session) - **(B)** — Book of Shadows (existing magical talent document) - **(S)** — SRD v2 Prima Materia (existing system document) - **(N)** — New this session (novel extraction with no prior source)

**Status Legend:** - **✅ PLACED** — Position confirmed, mechanics sketched - **🔧 NEEDS WORK** — Placed but mechanics need development - **⚠️ FLAGGED** — Placement or design conflict requiring resolution - **📋 STUB** — Named and placed, no mechanics yet

# <a name="_heading=h.nq9iubvzua5l"></a>**CATEGORY 1: FOUNDATIONAL COMBAT STYLES (8 Trees)**
These define HOW a character fights. Each has a Crux talent as the entry point.

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.h3jze9ffyc4v"></a>**TREE 1: TWO-HANDED STYLE**
**Crux: Power Attack** [Mundane, Active] (F) Trade accuracy for damage when wielding a two-handed weapon. Scaling penalty/bonus.
### <a name="_heading=h.e91mup5hfqf1"></a>**Direct Droplets (enhance Power Attack):**
•            **Furious Focus** [Mundane, Passive] (F) — Negate the Power Attack penalty on the first attack of your turn.

•            **Shattering Strike** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Spend Stamina when using Power Attack to deal bonus damage to objects, armor, and constructs.

•            **Cornugon Smash** [Mundane, Active] (F) — When you hit with a Power Attack, attempt a free Intimidate check against the target. *(Cross-tags: [Intimidation])*

•            **Vital Strike** [Mundane, Active] (F) — As a standard action, make one attack at your highest bonus dealing additional weapon damage dice. Scales in three tiers (Vital → Improved → Greater).

•            **Devastating Strike** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Vital Strike upgrade. Add a flat bonus to damage equal to weapon damage dice × 2.
### <a name="_heading=h.f0c7tzngdlt4"></a>**Area Attack Sub-Tree (branch off Power Attack):**
•            **Cleave** [Mundane, Active] (F) — When you hit with a melee attack, make a second attack against an adjacent target at a penalty.

•            **Great Cleave** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Cleave continues as long as you keep hitting adjacent targets.

•            **Cleaving Finish** [Mundane, Active] (F) — When you drop a target, make a free Cleave attack.

•            **Whirlwind Attack** [Mundane, Active] (F) — As a full action, make one melee attack against every opponent within your reach. Capstone of the Area Attack chain.
### <a name="_heading=h.x7wmlr440gn1"></a>**Juggernaut Sub-Tree (branch off Power Attack):**
•            **Improved Shove** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Enhanced Shove (Bull Rush) combat maneuver checks when using Power Attack.

•            **Wrecking Blow** [Mundane, Active] (F) — After a successful Shove, target provokes reaction attacks from your allies.

•            **Awesome Blow** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Send an opponent flying backward on a successful power strike. *(Cross-tags: [Reposition])*

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.czpct0ogs9hk"></a>**TREE 2: FINESSE STYLE**
**Crux: Weapon Finesse** [Mundane, Passive] (F) Use Dexterity/Agility instead of Strength for attack rolls with light or finesse weapons.
### <a name="_heading=h.xezmcrnbgi7e"></a>**Dueling Style Sub-Tree:**
•            **Dueling Style (Adept)** [Mundane, Passive] (F) — Requires one-handed weapon, other hand free. Add Dexterity/Agility modifier to damage rolls.

•            **Dueling Style (Expert)** [Mundane, Reactive] (F) — Significant bonus to Initiative. When an enemy misses you with a melee attack, use your reaction to counterattack.

•            **Dueling Style (Master)** [Mundane, Active] (F) — When you successfully counterattack, you may forgo damage to automatically disarm the opponent.
### <a name="_heading=h.p89fcvvgzuwv"></a>**Parry & Riposte Sub-Tree:**
•            **Opportune Parry** [Mundane, Reactive] (F/C) — Spend Stamina to use your reaction to parry an incoming melee attack. Roll your attack; if it exceeds the enemy’s, the attack misses.

•            **Riposte** [Mundane, Reactive] (F/C) — When you successfully parry, make an immediate counterattack as part of the same reaction.
### <a name="_heading=h.vjsvzwoddp3n"></a>**Evasive Footwork Sub-Tree:**

# <a name="_heading=h.31vra36lki2f"></a>**CATEGORY 3: TACTICAL / GENERAL (7+ Trees)**
These define WHAT you do with your combat actions beyond basic attacking and defending.

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.eak4p539mm1"></a>**TREE 12: COMBAT MANEUVERS (Meta-Tree with 10+ Sub-Trees)**
Each combat maneuver has its own Improved → Greater chain. These are listed by maneuver type.
### <a name="_heading=h.5w2qa4bffirz"></a>**Trip:**
•            **Improved Trip** [Mundane, Active] (F) — No AoO provoked. Bonus to Trip checks.

•            **Greater Trip** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Target provokes AoO from allies when tripped.
### <a name="_heading=h.1jemg0l3q1ap"></a>**Grapple:**
•            **Improved Grapple** [Mundane, Active] (F) — No AoO. Bonus to Grapple checks.

•            **Greater Grapple** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Maintain grapple as move action.

•            **Chokehold** [Mundane, Active] (F) — While grappling, can cut off air supply.

•            **Body Shield** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Use grappled opponent as cover.
### <a name="_heading=h.u8s28dd870ek"></a>**Disarm:**
•            **Improved Disarm** [Mundane, Active] (F) — No AoO. Bonus to Disarm checks.

•            **Greater Disarm** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Disarmed weapon lands 15 feet away.

•            **Disarming Strike** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Free Disarm on a critical hit.
### <a name="_heading=h.qav5ytat824n"></a>**Sunder:**
•            **Improved Sunder** [Mundane, Active] (F) — No AoO. Bonus to Sunder checks.

•            **Greater Sunder** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Excess damage carries through to wielder.

•            **Shattering Strike** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Bonus to Sunder against specific material types.
### <a name="_heading=h.qi7peu9dtlcr"></a>**Shove (Bull Rush):**
•            **Improved Shove** [Mundane, Active] (F) — No AoO. Bonus to Shove checks.

•            **Greater Shove** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Target provokes AoO from allies when shoved.

•            **Hurricane Punch** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Free Shove on successful unarmed attack.
### <a name="_heading=h.be2wypocjac"></a>**Feint:**
•            **Improved Feint** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Feint as a move action instead of standard.

•            **Greater Feint** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Feinted target is flat-footed to all attackers, not just you.
### <a name="_heading=h.kt7vor394d9s"></a>**Dirty Trick:**
•            **Improved Dirty Trick** [Mundane, Active] (F) — No AoO. Bonus to Dirty Trick checks.

•            **Greater Dirty Trick** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Condition from Dirty Trick lasts additional rounds.
### <a name="_heading=h.dkeu8gamk12z"></a>**Drag:**
•            **Improved Drag** [Mundane, Active] (F) — No AoO. Bonus to Drag checks.
### <a name="_heading=h.1eokpiluoam"></a>**Reposition:**
•            **Improved Reposition** [Mundane, Active] (F) — No AoO. Bonus to Reposition checks.
### <a name="_heading=h.mh4oc413wyx4"></a>**Steal:**
•            **Improved Steal** [Mundane, Active] (F) — No AoO. Bonus to Steal checks.

*Note: All Combat Maneuver talents scale with item tier (weapon properties that grant maneuver bonuses scale at Novice/Journeyman/Master from the SRD v2 weapon property system).*

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.j79323rog703"></a>**TREE 13: CRITICAL**
Two branches: Precision (surgical) and Brutal (overwhelming).
### <a name="_heading=h.k4lee738jg2f"></a>**Precision Branch:**
•            **Critical Focus** [Mundane, Passive] (F) — Bonus to critical confirmation rolls.

•            **Bleeding Critical** [Mundane, Passive] (F) — Confirmed crits cause bleed damage.

•            **Staggering Critical** [Mundane, Passive] (F) — Confirmed crits stagger the target.

•            **Disarming Strike** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Free Disarm on critical. *(Cross-tags: [Combat Maneuver (Disarm)])*
### <a name="_heading=h.u1uhhadyftr4"></a>**Brutal Branch:**
•            **Impact Critical** [Mundane, Passive] (F) — Crits with bludgeoning weapons push target back.

•            **Impaling Critical** [Mundane, Passive] (F) — Crits with piercing weapons impale, dealing ongoing damage.

•            **Exploit Weakness** [Mundane, Passive] (C: Samurai/high-tier) — Critical hits ignore Soak (DR).

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.r7gf8w290rfl"></a>**TREE 14: COMMANDER / AURA**
Three branches: Vow of Enmity, Battlefield Tactician, Aura of Command.
### <a name="_heading=h.2kr18j5au483"></a>**Vow of Enmity Branch (5 entry points, shared progression):**
**Entry Points (choose one — determines source and keying attribute):** - **Smite (Divine Mark)** [Infused, Swift] (C: Paladin) — Mark fueled by divine isotope resonance. Key: Schema divine score 60+. - **Quarry (Instinctual Mark)** [Mundane, Swift] (C: Ranger) — Mark from creature/terrain knowledge. Key: Wits/Intellect + Knowledge ranks. - **Challenge (Emotional Mark)** [Mundane, Swift] (C: Cavalier) — Mark from personal honor commitment. Key: Presence/Composure. - **Studied Combat (Analytical Mark)** [Mundane, Move→Swift] (C: Investigator) — Mark from real-time tactical analysis. Key: Intellect + Knowledge check. *(Quick Study droplet reduces action to Swift.)* - **Bane (Categorical Mark)** [Infused, Swift] (C: Inquisitor) — Mark applied to entire creature type via Knowledge check. Breadth trades for reduced per-target intensity.

**Shared Progression (all entry points feed into):** - **Tier 2 — Commander’s Judgment** [Mundane/Infused, Passive] (F/C) — Vow also analyzes target’s defenses. Ignore a portion of target’s Soak. - **Tier 3 — Overwhelming Assault** [Mundane/Infused, Active] (F/C) — Allies also gain a portion of your attack bonus against the Vow target. - **Capstone — Unbreakable Vow** [Mundane/Infused, Passive] (F/C) — Maintain two Vows simultaneously. Regain Stamina on defeating a Vow target.
### <a name="_heading=h.xtussgbdo19c"></a>**Battlefield Tactician Branch:**
•            **Coordinated Action (Tier 1)** [Mundane, Passive] (F/C) — Master one Teamwork talent. Always considered to have an ally with that talent for activation purposes.

•            **Tactician (Tier 2)** [Mundane, Active] (F/C) — Spend Stamina to grant one Teamwork talent to allies within 30ft for several rounds.

•            **Solo Tactics / Tactical Intuition (Tier 3)** [Mundane, Passive] (F/C: Inquisitor/Cavalier) — BIDIRECTIONAL: Your Teamwork talents function as if adjacent allies possess them, AND you possess any Teamwork talent an adjacent ally has. ✅ MERGED

•            **Master Tactician (Capstone)** [Mundane, Swift] (F/C) — Use Tactician as a swift action. Share two Teamwork talents simultaneously.
### <a name="_heading=h.9h7a9rff5isc"></a>**Aura of Command Branch:**
•            **Aura of Courage (Tier 1)** [Mundane/Infused, Passive] (F/C) — 10ft aura. Allies gain bonus on saves vs fear.

•            **Banner (Tier 2)** [Mundane, Active] (F/C) — Plant a banner creating a larger aura with attack or save bonuses.

•            **Inspiring Presence (Tier 3)** [Infused, Active] (F/C) — Spend Stamina per round. Allies gain scaling bonus to attack, damage, or skill checks.

•            **Stance Projection (Capstone)** [Infused, Active] (C: Skald) — Project your current active Stance to willing allies within aura radius. Allies gain the stance’s benefits and penalties. Can accept or decline each round. ✅ MERGED (replaces specific “Raging Song” capstone with generalized version)

•            **Contingent Expression** [Magical, Active — Prep Phase] (C: Mesmerist) — During rest, spend ÆP to implant one triggered expression in a willing ally. Fires automatically when conditions are met. Uses YOUR stats. Maintain up to Dominion modifier simultaneously. ✅ PLACED

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.t9w1bn1y5j3w"></a>**TREE 15: INTIMIDATION & FEAR**
•            **Dazzling Display (Crux)** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Full action. Intimidate all enemies within 30ft using a weapon display.

•            **Cornugon Smash** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Free Intimidate on a Power Attack hit. *(Cross-tags: [Two-Handed Style])*

•            **Shatter Defenses** [Mundane, Passive] (F) — Frightened or shaken targets are flat-footed to your attacks.

•            **Gory Finish** [Mundane, Active] (F) — When you drop a foe, free Intimidate vs all enemies who witnessed it.

•            **Fearsome Might** [Mundane, Passive] (F) — Use Strength instead of Charisma for Intimidate checks.

•            **Dreadful Carnage** [Mundane, Active] (F) — When you reduce a foe to 0 HP, free Dazzling Display.

•            **Intimidating Prowess** [Mundane, Passive] (F) — Add Strength modifier to Intimidate checks.

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.fhbpwzr2pn0w"></a>**TREE 16: PRECISION DAMAGE**
**Crux: Sneak Attack / Precision Damage** [Mundane, Active] (F/C) Deal extra precision damage dice when target is flanked, flat-footed, or otherwise denied defenses.

•            **Analytical Approach** [Mundane, Move→Swift] (C: Investigator) — Alternative activation: study a target (Knowledge check + move action) to apply Precision Damage without requiring flanking or surprise. Quick Study droplet reduces to swift. ✅ PLACED

•            **Studied Strike** [Mundane, Active] (C: Investigator) — Cash out Analytical Approach for burst precision damage, ending the study. *(Re-studying costs Inspiration/FP.)*

•            **Debilitating Strike** [Mundane, Active] (F/C) — When you deal Precision Damage, spend Stamina to apply a debilitating penalty (speed, attacks, or defense).

•            **Bleeding Attack** [Mundane, Passive] (F/C) — Precision Damage causes bleed.

•            **Crippling Strike** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Precision Damage also applies Strength damage.

•            **Master Strike** [Mundane, Active] (F/C) — Capstone. Once per day, Precision Damage forces save vs paralysis or death.

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.bjb5xlkzmhul"></a>**TREE 17: TEAMWORK**
Talents that require coordination. Solo Tactics / Tactical Intuition (Commander tree) allows these to function without trained allies.

•            **Paired Opportunists** [Mundane, Passive] (F) — When ally provokes AoO from a flanked enemy, you also get an AoO.

•            **Precise Strike (Teamwork)** [Mundane, Passive] (F) — When flanking, deal extra precision damage. *(Distinct from Daredevil’s Precise Strike.)*

•            **Outflank** [Mundane, Passive] (F) — Increased flanking bonus. On a crit, flanking partner gets an AoO.

•            **Broken Wing Gambit** [Mundane, Reactive] (F) — Take a penalty to defense. When enemy hits you, all allies with this talent get an AoO.

•            **Coordinated Charge** [Mundane, Reactive] (F) — When an ally charges, you can immediately charge the same target.

•            **Swap Places** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Switch positions with adjacent ally.

•            **Escape Route** [Mundane, Passive] (F) — No AoO for movement when adjacent to ally with this talent.

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.gdi7v01t23en"></a>**TREE 18: AREA ATTACK**
*(Note: Also functions as a sub-tree off Power Attack in Two-Handed Style. Cross-referenced here for characters who access it through other paths.)*

See Tree 1, Area Attack Sub-Tree (Cleave → Great Cleave → Cleaving Finish → Whirlwind Attack).

# <a name="_heading=h.atkdbthrhh7y"></a>**CATEGORY 4: ESOTERIC / RESOURCE (Revised)**
These trees bridge martial and magical investment, govern resource models, and handle non-standard combat systems.

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.u8sbjp916kf4"></a>**TREE 19: CHANNELING DISCIPLINE (NEW — from class extractions)**
Talents governing HOW magical expressions are delivered, recovered, and amplified. These are learned techniques, not innate abilities. Feed Tₘ.
### <a name="_heading=h.fxh0iw2sgq05"></a>**Conduit Channeling Path (C: Kineticist):**
•            **Conduit Channeling (Crux)** [Magical, Active] (N) — Accept voluntary Strain on a known isotope to reduce ÆP/Stamina cost of an expression using that isotope. Trade long-term mutation risk for short-term power.

•            **Planar Reserve** [Magical, Passive] (N) — During rest, pre-process and store Strain points equal to (Integrity modifier). Stored points can be spent in place of accepting live Strain.

•            **Planar Attunement** [Magical, Passive] (N) — While your Strain counter on any isotope is above 0, gain scaling bonus to expressions using that isotope. Higher Strain = bigger bonus = closer to mutation threshold.

•            **Focused Channeling** [Magical, Active] (N) — Spend a move/standard action to reduce ÆP or Stamina cost of your next expression by a scaling amount. If you take damage while gathering, concentration check or forced Strain.

•            **Ambient Expression** [Magical, Passive] (N) — When you have Strain on an isotope AND that isotope is at rank 7+, passively emanate the isotope’s interaction profile in a small radius. Allies and enemies affected. High-tier talent.

•            **Overexpression** [Magical, Active] (N) — Exceed the normal ÆP/Strain limits of a single expression for dramatically increased effect. Temporarily severs connection to that isotope until re-established (1 action). Capstone.
### <a name="_heading=h.k3y596vz5afh"></a>**Channeled Strike Path (C: Magus):**
•            **Channeled Strike (Crux)** [Magical, Active] (N) — Deliver one expression through a melee weapon attack using the weapon’s attack roll. Expression must use an isotope at rank 3+. Cost: expression’s ÆP + Stamina surcharge by ESL. Requires reset action before reuse. On crit, expression also crits.

•            **Resonant Stance** [Magical, Stance] (N) — After Channeled Strike or casting, enter stance. Weapon strikes deal bonus damage equal to (isotope rank / 2) of the last channeled isotope’s damage type.

•            **Improvised Infusion** [Magical, Swift] (N) — Spend ÆP to temporarily apply one expression effect to weapon. Duration: minutes = isotope rank. No slot occupied. *(Subsumes Arcane Pool weapon enhancement.)*

•            **Flowing Channel** [Magical, Passive] (N) — When you cast an expression with ESL 2 or lower through normal casting, Channeled Strike recharges immediately without reset action.

•            **Reactive Channel** [Magical, Reactive] (N) — When a creature you threaten begins an expression, spend reaction + Stamina for Channeled Strike against them. On hit + damage, target must concentrate or lose expression. High-tier, requires Combat Reflexes.

•            **Ætheric Charge** [Magical, Active] (N) — Channeled Strike variant during Charge action. Bonus damage from momentum. *(Subsumed from standalone feat-derived talent.)*
### <a name="_heading=h.5lam97et6sxm"></a>**General Channeling Talents:**
•            **Disciplined Focus** [Magical, Active] (C: Psionics) — Spend full-round action to achieve focused state. While focused: passive bonus to one defense pillar = Integrity modifier. Expend focus (free action) for one of: add Integrity to a d20 roll, negate a condition for 1 round, or reduce next expression’s ÆP cost by Integrity. Multiple talents can read the focused/unfocused state.

•            **Reckless Channeling** [Magical, Active] (C: Psionics/Wilder) — Increase effective ESL cap by 1-3 beyond Tri-Tier limit. Per point of increase: cumulative 15% chance of Channeling Backlash (dazed 1 round, lose ÆP, expend Focus).

•            **Swift Self-Channel** [Magical, Swift] (C: Warpriest) — Cast a self-targeted expression as a swift action by spending additional ÆP equal to the expression’s ESL.

•            **Reactive Modification** [Magical, Active — at moment of casting] (C: Psychic) — Maintain a secondary pool (Integrity mod + relevant attribute mod) spent ONLY at moment of expression release. Each point applies one minor modification from a menu determined by isotope profile (maximize one die, self-heal, persist effect, extend targeting, add secondary damage type).

•            **Practiced Rote Mastery** [Magical, Passive] (C: Witch) — Signature Rotes with ESL 2 or below: ÆP cost drops to 0. Each Mastered Rote can only affect a specific target once per day.

•            **Rote Compression** [Magical, Passive] (C: Psychic) — Voluntarily reduce a Signature Rote’s ESL by 1-3 steps, proportionally reducing ÆP cost and effect power. Deploy high-investment rotes flexibly across the power spectrum.

•            **Wandering Attunement** [Magical, Active — Prep Phase] (C: Shaman) — During rest, temporarily attune to one isotope you DON’T have ranked, treating it as rank 1 for the day. Gain access to basic expressions and one Mastered Rote from its natural effect list. No Strain, no mutation. Prerequisite: any two isotopes at rank 5+.

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.rp1fa09ixdxq"></a>**TREE 20: METAMAGIC (from Book of Shadows)**
Each is a learned technique that modifies expression construction. All cost additional ÆP. All feed Tₘ.

•            **Empower Spell** [Magical, Active] (B) — +2 ÆP. Increase all variable numeric effects by 50%.

•            **Maximize Spell** [Magical, Active] (B) — +4 ÆP. All variable numeric effects at maximum value.

•            **Quicken Spell** [Magical, Active] (B) — +(ESL × 2) ÆP. Cast standard-action expression as swift.

•            **Enlarge Spell** [Magical, Active] (B) — +1 ÆP. Double range.

•            **Widen Spell** [Magical, Active] (B) — +2 ÆP. Double area of effect.

•            **Extend Spell** [Magical, Active] (B) — +1 ÆP. Double duration.

•            **Unbalanced Magic** [Magical, Active] (B) — Two maneuvers: Overwhelm (penalty to attack, equal bonus to DC/damage) or Pinpoint (penalty to damage/DC, equal bonus to attack).

*Architect Efficiency:* Metamagic surcharges reduced by ⌊Intellect ÷ 2⌋ each, minimum +1 per metamagic. (B) *Improviser Affinity:* Base ÆP per spell reduced by (Anima - 3), minimum 0. Metamagic surcharges paid at full rate. (B)

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.sesj7rgmjqp6"></a>**TREE 21: FOCUS TALENTS (from Book of Shadows)**
Passive specialization bonuses. Feed Tₘ.

•            **Focus (Effect)** [Magical, Passive] (B) — Choose one Effect (e.g., Affliction). All expressions using this Effect have +1 save DC. Can be taken multiple times.

•            **Focus (Subtype/Isotope)** [Magical, Passive] (B) — Choose one isotope. All expressions using this isotope have +1 save DC. Can be taken multiple times.

•            **Spell Penetration** [Magical, Passive] (B) — Bonus on checks to overcome magical resistance or Nullify effects.

•            **Warden Breaker** [Magical, Passive] (B) — Significant bonus to overcome static magical defenses (Nullify effects, Deepguard wards).

•            **Immunity Breach** [Magical, Active] (B) — Prerequisite: high Overall Mastery. Spend significant additional ÆP to temporarily suppress a creature’s Immunity to a specific isotope for one round.

•            **Expanded Consciousness** [Magical, Passive] (B) — Adds (OMT × 3) to Mastery Capacity Pool. Can be purchased up to OMT times. *(Governs companion capacity, sustained expression capacity, Identity Manifestation capacity.)*

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.ij1she41be6g"></a>**TREE 22: MUTATION (Scaffolding — contents are future mutation catalogue candidates)**
### <a name="_heading=h.dbo3i5ct8dx2"></a>**Primal Aspect (C: Hunter):**
•            **Primal Aspect** [Supernatural, Swift] (N) — Verdant Font Fauna isotope at rank 3+. Manifest minor physical adaptations as a swift action. Choose from menu determined by Fauna isotope rank. Switch as swift action. At rank 7+, manifest two simultaneously. Entry point for voluntary temporary micro-mutation. ⚠️ [Supernatural] tag — mutation catalogue candidate.
### <a name="_heading=h.kbpj9x8d0x7x"></a>**Innate Expression (C: Oracle — Character Creation, not talent tree):**
•            **Innate Expression** [Supernatural, Passive] (N) — Character creation option. Permanent, irremovable paired detriment/benefit. Detriment constant; benefit scales with investment thresholds. Generates 1 FP per session as pattern break. Produces a mandatory behavioral signature (leitmotif). ⚠️ [Supernatural] tag — mutation catalogue candidate.
### <a name="_heading=h.5xwxnkwu9pxk"></a>**Identity Manifestation (C: Psionics):**
•            **Identity Manifestation** [Supernatural, Swift] (N) — Designate one known expression (typically Summon weapon/armor) as an Identity Manifestation. ÆP cost permanently 0. Call/dismiss as swift action. Scales with isotope rank + effect tier. No material substrate (no slots, no routing). Can only have ONE. Prerequisite: Summon effect Tier 3+, relevant isotope rank 5+. ⚠️ [Supernatural] tag — mutation catalogue candidate.

*Note: All Totemic Path talents (Durability tree) are also [Supernatural] tagged and will transfer to the mutation catalogue in the separation pass.*

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.usl5qp6y0re6"></a>**TREE 23: SŌMATURGY (Partially deferred)**
Contains the self-transformation and external construct systems. Identified Crux paths:

•            **Forge Construct (Crux)** [Magical/Supernatural] (F/C) — Create an external construct companion via Summon effect. Evolution Pool for customization. *(Overlaps with Companion system, Summons acquisition path.)*

•            **Shape Soulmeld (Crux)** [Magical/Supernatural] (F/C) — Invest Stamina into semi-permanent self-enhancements (Soulmelds) that occupy body slots. From Akashic Mysteries source.

•            **Spell Combat integration** — 🔧 How does casting + fighting in the same turn work at the system level? Channeled Strike (Magus extraction) provides the weapon-delivery path. But what about casting a non-touch expression + making a weapon attack in the same round? This is the unresolved action economy question.

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.ep7y077twjlf"></a>**TREE 24: COMPANION SYSTEM (Two-Axis)**
### <a name="_heading=h.wzvrdqh2d5l3"></a>**Axis 1 — Acquisition Path (choose one):**
**Contracts (Binding):** - **Magical Contract (Crux)** [Magical, Active — Ritual] (Companion Doc) — Create magically binding contracts. Cost: Stamina + ÆP = target EML. Counts against Mastery Capacity Pool. - Ironclad Oath, Binding Mark, Contractual Sense (Novice) (Companion Doc) - Strengthened Chains, Binding Circle (Adept) (Companion Doc) - Unbreakable Vow (Master) (Companion Doc) - Contractual Enhancement tree (Companion Doc) - Contract Versatility tree (Companion Doc)

**Empathy (Bonding):** - **Empathetic Bond (Crux)** [Mundane→Magical, Active — Ritual] (Companion Doc) — Form permanent empathetic bond with willing creature. Silent communication of emotions and simple concepts. - Bond Enhancement tree (Companion Doc) - Shared Abilities tree (Companion Doc)

**Psychic Contagion (needs rename):** - **Mental Domination (Crux)** [Magical, Active] (Companion Doc) — Compel creature through psychic force. ⚠️ Ethically restricted under guild law for sapient creatures.

**Summons:** - **Conjured Companion (Crux)** [Magical, Active] (Companion Doc) — Magically create or call a companion. Includes Construct Customization (C: Summoner) — apply mutations from menu determined by isotope investment, reconfigurable on resummon.
### <a name="_heading=h.cpjdxo5xpb99"></a>**Axis 2 — Creature Type Specialization (choose one+):**
Beast (Wild), Arcaniform (Arcanist), Anomalous (Harbinger), Floraform (Verdant), Forged (Artificer), Humanoid (Command), Interstitial (Binder), Returned (Gravemaster)

*(Each has Novice/Adept/Master talent tiers per the Companion Archetype document.)*
### <a name="_heading=h.ipbnq96ynwp9"></a>**Cross-Path Talents (available to any acquisition path):**
•            **Proxy Channeling** [Magical, Active] (C: Wizard/Witch) — Deliver touch-range expressions through companion. Uses YOUR stats, companion’s position.

•            **Sense Sharing** [Magical, Active] (C: Wizard/Witch/Psionics) — Perceive through companion’s senses.

•            **Synthesis** [Supernatural, Active] (C: Summoner/Spiritualist) — Merge companion into self. Gain subset of companion’s Feature Point investments as personal bonuses. ⚠️ [Supernatural] — mutation catalogue candidate.

•            **Full Synthesis** [Supernatural, Active] (C: Summoner) — Capstone. Transform into hybrid of self and companion. ⚠️ [Supernatural] — mutation catalogue candidate.

•            **Handler’s Shield** [Mundane, Reactive] — Take damage that would hit your companion instead.

•            **Living Archive** [Magical, Passive] (C: Witch/Shaman) — Companion stores rotes/reagents/isotope expressions. Access through bond. Lose access if companion lost.

•            **Companion Resonance** [Magical/Mundane, Passive] — Passive skill/save bonus while companion is within 30ft. Bonus determined by creature type.

•            **Focus Sharing** [Magical/Mundane, Active] (C: Hunter) — Apply your active Primal Aspect or self-buff to companion.

•            **Teamwork Sharing** [Mundane, Passive] (C: Hunter) — Companion benefits from your Teamwork talents as if it possessed them.

•            **Raise Companion** [Magical, Active — Ritual] (C: Hunter) — Spend significant resources to revive dead bonded companion through the bond.

•            **Dual-State Companion** [Supernatural, Swift] (C: Spiritualist) — Toggle companion between incorporeal and physical states. ⚠️ [Supernatural] — mutation catalogue candidate.

# <a name="_heading=h.ayprurlmsuqb"></a>**CATEGORY 5: GENERAL POOL**
Talents that don’t belong to a specific tree. Available to any character meeting prerequisites.

-----
## <a name="_heading=h.tpfiezgmh68b"></a>**Daredevil Engine (Grit & Panache):**
•            **Grit & Panache (Crux)** [Mundane, Passive] (F/C) — Gain a Stamina Pool. Core methods of regaining Stamina: critical hits and defeating foes. Entry point for the Deed system.

•            **Charmed Life** [Mundane, Reactive] (F/C) — Spend Stamina as reaction to add bonus to any saving throw.

•            **Cheat Death** [Mundane, Reactive] (F/C) — When reduced to 0 HP, spend significant Stamina to be reduced to 1 HP instead.

•            **Deeds (à la carte):** Each is a separate talent purchased individually.

–            *Offensive:* Deadeye (ranged touch), Precise Strike (finesse damage), Targeting Shot (debuff specific body part)

–            *Defensive:* Opportune Parry & Riposte (see Finesse tree), Gunslinger’s Dodge

–            *Mobility:* Kip-Up (stand from prone as swift)

⚠️ **FLAG: The Daredevil engine has been wanting its own tree since the April 10 session. It’s currently in General Pool but may deserve promotion to a 9th Foundational Style or a 4th Defensive Philosophy. The Stamina recovery-on-performance mechanic is unique enough to warrant independent tree status.**
## <a name="_heading=h.hrht4uerwp5r"></a>**Invoked Aspect (C: Inquisitor):**
•            **Invoked Aspect** [Magical/Infused, Swift] (N) — Invoke one aspect of a god whose Schema dimension you score 60+ on. Scaling combat modifier appropriate to that god’s portfolio. Switch as swift action. Menu derived from YOUR Schema scores. At higher tiers: invoke two, then three aspects simultaneously. ✅ INDEPENDENT — no overlap with existing feat-derived talents.
## <a name="_heading=h.vbjipbsds74j"></a>**Wielder Attunement (C: Occultist):**
•            **Wielder Attunement** [Magical, Active — Prep Phase] (N) — During rest, invest ÆP from personal pool into items you possess. Invested ÆP produces resonant bonus determined by item’s dominant isotope profile. Supplements item’s drip baseline.

•            **Field Reallocation** [Magical, Active] (N) — Transfer invested ÆP between items. 10 minutes, 1-point loss per transfer.
## <a name="_heading=h.l7bcfb9bt6is"></a>**Legacy Channeling (C: Medium):**
•            **Legacy Channeling (Crux)** [Magical, Active — Ritual] (N) — 1-hour ritual. Attune to a soul stone containing another entity’s experiential record. Gain limited access to one category of the channeled soul’s talent investment (at reduced effectiveness) for the day. Prerequisite: Palimpsest Echo isotope rank 5+, possession of viable soul stone.

•            **Legacy Accord** [Magical, Passive] (N) — When channeling, adopt one of the channeled soul’s Instincts. While honored: gain (Integrity mod) free Legacy Surges without contamination. Violation: lose all remaining free surges + immediate contamination.

•            *Schema Contamination* — inherent risk mechanic, not a talent. Each heavy draw advances contamination. Thresholds: behavioral bleed → temporary possession.

•            *Legacy Surge* — inherent to Legacy Channeling. Add channeled soul’s relevant Schema die to a d20 check. Each use advances contamination by 1.
## <a name="_heading=h.wcxa0oxjrgyo"></a>**Analytical Focus / FP Distance 0 (C: Investigator):**
•            **Professional Expertise** [Mundane, Passive] (N) — FP cost for Distance 0 operations in trained skill domains drops to 0. Add relevant Schema dimension die to personal d20 checks in areas of professional competence at no FP cost. Prerequisite: 5+ ranks in any skill. ✅ PLACED — clarifies the FP Distance 0 mechanic.
## <a name="_heading=h.o4znbwfi6ngh"></a>**Constructed Identity (C: Vigilante):**
•            **Constructed Identity** [Mundane, Active] (N) — Maintain a secondary social profile (Poise, Clout, reputation) mechanically separate from primary. Social consequences accumulate independently. Switching: 10 minutes + disguise prep. Investigation TN based on Skullduggery skill. Prerequisite: Skullduggery 5+, Deception 5+. ✅ PLACED — social pillar talent.
## <a name="_heading=h.o7pfaobglzai"></a>**Exploit Weakness / Kirin Strike:**
•            **Exploit Weakness** [Mundane, Swift] (F) — Make a Knowledge check against a visible creature. On success: add Intellect modifier to damage against that creature for 1 round.
## <a name="_heading=h.eoicpfgtlysm"></a>**Weapon Training:**
•            **Weapon Training** [Mundane, Passive] (F/C) — Choose a weapon group. Scaling Competence bonus to attack and damage. Tiers: Focus → Specialization → Greater → Mastery.
## <a name="_heading=h.bm70d1ckuxjf"></a>**Martial Flexibility (C: Brawler):**
•            **Barroom Brawler (Tier 1)** [Mundane, Active] (F/C) — Once per day, spend Stamina to temporarily gain one martial talent you don’t possess but meet prerequisites for. Short duration.

•            **Martial Flexibility (Tier 2)** [Mundane, Active] (F/C) — More frequent use, longer duration.

•            **Effortless Flexibility (Capstone)** [Mundane, Swift] (F/C) — Use as swift action. Adapt fighting style mid-combat.
## <a name="_heading=h.vt8vc15w384r"></a>**Casting Methodology (System Rule, not a talent):**
Four channeling methods with different detection and vulnerability profiles: - **External** — Visible gestures, audible incantations. Blocked by restraint/silence. - **Internal** — No external tells. Blocked by emotional suppression/mental disruption. - **Somatic** — Physical motion integrated into combat. Blocked by immobilization. - **Substrate** — Routed through weapon/implement. Blocked by disarming/sundering.

# <a name="_heading=h.l139351icwb3"></a>**CATEGORY 6: ARTIFICE PATHS (from SRD v2)**
Five crafting talent paths, each with Novice/Journeyman/Master tiers. Already defined in SRD v2 Prima Materia.

•            **Path 1: Mundane Artisan** (S) — Physical operations. Masterwork → Efficient Production → Signature Creation.

•            **Path 2: Appraiser** (S) — Analysis and valuation. Material Reading → Component Analysis → Full Spectrum Analysis.

•            **Path 3: Ætheric Scrivener** (S) — Documentation and inscription. Scribe’s Hand → Runic Inscription → Schematics Master.

•            **Path 4: Alchemist** (S) — Alchemical operations. Reagent Brewer → Process Alchemist → Essence Alchemist.

•            **Path 5: Enchanter** (S) — Component installation and routing. Artificer’s Eye → Architecture Design → Forge Architect.

*(Full mechanical details in SRD v2 Prima Materia. Not duplicated here.)*

A collection of files that may or may not connect in ways that make narrative sense. This is a raw dump of ideas.
